Abstract: Landmark mathematical formalizations have taken specialist teams years to complete. We present FormalFlow, a system that coordinates AI proving agents under human supervision to address statement drift and proof composition in long-horizon formalization. Drawing on software engineering principles and practices, it uses a shared blueprint to guide nested planning, proving and review loops. Agents strengthen verification and review throughout formalization. We completed a machine-checked Lean 4 proof of the quantum soundness of the classical low individual-degree test, a core theorem underlying MIP* = RE. Developing the proof took 63 days; greater parallelism could further reduce this time. The final library contains 126,367 lines of Lean code, all generated by agents. The formalization corrects side conditions and intermediate errors while preserving the published final error bound under corrected assumptions. This work provides a verified foundation for quantum complexity and demonstrates a route to affordable verification of major research proofs by small teams.

</details>


### [70] [Automated search for highly contextual Kochen-Specker proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19862)
*Axel Muller,Metod Saniga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以超图（hypergram）为抽象载体，系统枚举反交换图并为其关联唯一支撑超图，从而高效搜索高鲁棒性的可观测量型Kochen-Specker证明，将容忍误差$\varepsilon$从0.424提升至0.707。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可观测量型Kochen-Specker证明的鲁棒性由容忍误差$\varepsilon=2d/|H|$刻画，但传统搜索依赖于具体量子比特与Pauli标号，导致搜索空间冗余、效率低。作者希望剥离量子实现细节，仅基于底层抽象结构（超图与反交换图构成的hypergram）进行搜索，以获得更鲁棒的状态无关上下文性证明。

Method: 1. 将鲁棒性指标归约为仅依赖hypergram的量$\varepsilon=2d/|H|$；2. 先枚举反交换图$G$，再为每个$G$关联唯一的hypergram，其超图支撑为$HS(G)$，实现每个图只考察一个候选；3. 在House of Graphs数据库及至多24个顶点的顶点传递图普查上运行该流水线；4. 通过证明“图的每个完美匹配都是其线图的一个上下文”，刻画线图与图并集这两类主要来源；5. 在辛极空间中用ovoid、双曲二次曲面和Fano平面给出最好构型的有限几何描述。

Result: 该流水线复现了Peres-Mermin方阵、doily和Mermin五角星，并发现$\varepsilon$达到0.707的构型，显著超过此前发表的0.424记录。最优构型主要来自线图与图的并集；作者证明Peres-Mermin方阵与doily分别是两个无限族的前两个成员。

Conclusion: 将搜索空间从量子实现层面抽象到hypergram层面可大幅提升状态无关上下文性证明的鲁棒性搜索效率，并揭示最优构型与线图、图并集及辛极空间中经典有限几何对象的深层联系。

Abstract: Observable-based Kochen-Specker proofs are configurations of multi-qubit Pauli observables grouped into contexts whose products are plus or minus the indentity. Their robustness as state-independent contextuality tests can be measured by the tolerated error per context $\varepsilon = 2d/|H|$, where $d$ is the contextuality degree and $|H|$ the number of contexts. Since the degree depends only on an underlying abstract structure called the hypergram i.e. the pair formed by the context hypergraph and the anticommutation graph, the search for highly contextual proofs can be carried out on them instead, with no reference to qubits or to any particular Pauli labeling. We further exploit this by enumerating anticommutation graphs first, and then by associating to each graph $G$ the single hypergram carrying its entire hypergraph support $HS(G)$, so that exactly one candidate is examined per graph. Applied to the House of Graphs database and to censuses of vertex-transitive graphs on at most 24 vertices, this pipeline recovers the Peres-Mermin square, the doily and the Mermin pentagram, and yields configurations reaching $\varepsilon = 0.707$, against $0.424$ for the previous published record. The best configurations are predominantly those stemming from line graphs and unions of graphs; we explain the former by showing that every perfect matching of a graph is a context of its line graph, which exhibits the Peres-Mermin square and the doily as the first members of two infinite families. We close with finite geometric descriptions of the most striking configurations inside symplectic polar spaces, in terms of ovoids, hyperbolic quadrics and Fano planes.

</details>


### [71] [Quantum WalkScore: Benchmarking Quantum Computers on the Graph Nodefinding Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19931)
*Noé Olivier,Michel Nowak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向应用的量子基准测试 QWS，通过在图节点搜索任务中的最大可解问题规模来评估 NISQ 及未来容错量子计算机的性能，并在 IBM 多代真实量子处理器上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算硬件向容错方向快速发展，亟需在应用相关的量子算法上评估近期量子平台的实际能力，而现有基准多偏底层指标，缺乏面向应用的、可扩展的评测手段。

Method: 设计可扩展的应用导向基准 Quantum WalkScore（QWS），以离散时间量子行走与量子振幅放大为核心量子例程，求解图节点发现问题（标记顶点搜索）。QWS 通过打分方式量化性能，即找出在成功概率高于既定阈值条件下可求解的最大问题规模；同时基于无噪声模拟结果给出参数选择场景，并在多代 IBM 真实量子处理器上开展实验评测。

Result: 给出了完整的协议描述与示例参数选择方案，并通过多代 IBM 真实量子处理器的实验运行展示了 QWS 的评测流程与可行性。

Conclusion: QWS 提供了一种可扩展、面向应用且以最大可解问题规模为指标的量子计算基准，适用于 NISQ 设备与未来容错量子计算机的性能评估。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing hardware toward fault-tolerance have increased interest in evaluating near-term quantum platforms on application-relevant quantum algorithms. In this work, we introduce Quantum WalkScore (QWS), a scalable application-oriented benchmark designed to assess the performance of NISQ and future fault-tolerant quantum computers in executing essential quantum routines --discrete-time quantum walks and quantum amplitude amplification-- to solve the graph nodefinding problem (marked-vertex search). QWS quantifies performance by scoring the largest problem size for which a designated target node can be found with success probability above a defined threshold. In addition to the complete protocol description, we provide example parameter-selection scenarios designed from noiseless simulation results and demonstrate QWS evaluation through experimental runs on multiple generations of IBM real quantum processors.

</details>


### [72] [Quantum Graph Convolutional Networks: Implementation and Trainability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19983)
*Paul San Sebastian Sein,Theodor Iosif,Tilen G. Limbäck-Stokin,Kin Ian Lo,Yidong Liao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于Liao等人的量子图神经网络（QGNN）框架，实现了SGC与LGC两种量子图卷积模型，在开放基准图数据集和半监督任务上通过量子模拟进行评测，并与经典基线比较性能与优化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典GNN在大图上的训练与推理受限于内存瓶颈和稀疏线性代数计算负载，量子计算提供的原语可能改善图学习的可扩展性。

Method: 在QGNN框架下实现两种代表性架构——简化图卷积（SGC）与线性图卷积（LGC），使用量子模拟在开放基准图数据集及半监督学习任务上评测，并与经典基线进行预测性能和优化行为对比；同时进行代价梯度分析以确定模型可训练的任务范围，并开展经典可模拟性研究以寻找电路在训练中保持鲁棒的区域。

Result: 量子模型以更少参数达到与经典基线相竞争的性能；代价梯度分析明确了所展示模型可训练的任务；经典可模拟性研究给出了电路在训练中保持鲁棒的条件区间。

Conclusion: 量子图卷积模型在参数效率上具有优势，但其可训练性与鲁棒性依赖于特定任务与参数区间，需要结合代价梯度与可模拟性分析来确定适用边界。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve state-of-the-art performance on graph-structured data, but training and inference on large graphs are often bottlenecked by memory constraints and sparse linear-algebra workloads. Quantum computing offers an alternative set of primitives that may improve scalability for graph learning. Building on the quantum graph neural network (QGNN) framework of Liao \textit{et al.}, this work implements two representative architectures --- the Simplified Graph Convolution (SGC) and Linear Graph Convolution (LGC) models --- and evaluates them on open benchmark graph datasets and semi-supervised learning tasks using quantum simulation. We compare predictive performance and optimization behavior against classical baselines, showing that the quantum models achieve competitive performance with fewer parameters. Finally, we present a cost gradient analysis that identifies the tasks for which the models showcased are trainable. This is followed by a classical simulability study to find regimes in which the proposed circuits remain robust during training.

</details>


### [73] [Private communication from Pauli channels with no privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20133)
*Uthirakalyani G,Pritam Halder,David Elkouss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 两个各自的私密经典容量为零的Pauli双量子比特信道，联合使用时可传输私密信息，实现私密通信容量的激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典世界中信道容量是固定的，而量子理论使通信能力具有情境性。作者希望探究：单个无容量（私密经典容量为零）的量子信道组合后能否产生非零的私密通信能力，即容量的可激活性问题。

Method: 构造两个Pauli双量子比特信道：一个其环境可将接收方输出重构至矩阵转置；另一个为反退化（antidegradable）信道。利用由秩三Bell态混合构成的简单二进制编码来激活私密通信。核心构件是一个转置-反退化但非反退化的信道。

Result: 证明两个各自私密经典容量均为零的Pauli双量子比特信道联合使用后可传输私密信息；当第二个信道替换为50%量子比特擦除信道时，也得到类似结果。

Conclusion: 量子信道的私密经典容量具有可激活性（super-additivity），单信道为零并不意味着联合使用仍为零；转置-反退化信道可作为激活私密通信的关键资源。

Abstract: A channel capacity quantifies the communication capability of a noisy physical process. In contrast to communication channels in the classical world, quantum theory makes this capability contextual. We show that two Pauli two-qubit channels, each with zero private classical capacity, can be used to transmit private information when used together. One is a two-qubit Pauli channel whose environment can reconstruct the receiver output up to matrix transposition; the other is an antidegradable channel. We obtain a similar result when the second channel is the 50% qubit erasure channel. A simple binary code built from rank-three mixtures of Bell states activates private communication. The main ingredient in our construction is a transpose-antidegradable channel that is not antidegradable.

</details>


### [74] [Electrostatic Stabilization of Near-Surface Quantum Sensors via Dielectric Interface Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20137)
*Atharva Paranjape,Kathrin Küster,Olga Shevstova,Lisa Ebo,Toni Hache,Klaus Kern,Rainer Stöhr,Jörg Wrachtrup,Aparajita Singha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用TiO2介电界面工程稳定近超真空条件下浅层NV色心的电荷态，NV-布居提升79%，自旋共振对比度增强45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近表面量子缺陷的电荷态稳定性对纳米尺度传感至关重要，但在超高真空（UHV）下，表面诱导的能带弯曲会破坏具有计量价值的电荷态，这一问题尤为突出，亟需一种稳健且可重复的稳定方法。

Method: 在近UHV条件（P = 3×10⁻⁹ mbar）下，对浅层植入（<10 nm深）的NV色心进行介电界面工程，即在金刚石表面施加TiO2涂层，并对可单独寻址的NV色心开展测量与相干控制实验。

Result: TiO2涂层抑制了表面诱导的静电场，室温下NV-布居增加79%，NV自旋共振对比度提升45%；相干控制测量进一步显示电荷态转换动力学被抑制。

Conclusion: 介电屏蔽是一种强大且可重复的策略，可在极端条件下的扫描探针兼容几何构型中调控NV色心的电荷跃迁能级。

Abstract: Control of charge-state stability in near-surface quantum defects is critical for nanoscale sensing, yet remains particularly challenging under ultra-high vacuum (UHV), where surface-induced band bending destabilizes the metrologically relevant charge-state. Here, we present a robust and reproducible approach for stabilizing shallowly implanted (< 10 nm deep) nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in near-UHV conditions (P = $3 \times 10^{-9}$ mbar) based on dielectric interface engineering. Through measurements on individually addressable NV centers, we demonstrate that a TiO2 coating on the diamond suppresses surface-induced electrostatic fields, yielding a 79% increase n NV- population and a 45% enhancement in NV-spin resonance contrast at room temperature. Coherent control measurements further reveal suppressed charge-state conversion dynamics. These results establish dielectric screening as a powerful and reproducible strategy to engineer charge transition energetics of NV centers in scanning-probe-compatible geometries under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Exceptional points and Jordan-chain signatures in quantum first-passage statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20148)
*Lachlan Bridges*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究监测量子系统中首次通过可观测量的异常点(EP)特征，提出其由两个独立生存机制支配：非线性谱缺陷性须传递到物理单能级首次通过阶梯，且阶梯的Jordan模须与制备态和末态观测有非零交叠。作者证明阈值因子化H_N(s)=R_s^N、识别物理Perron分支、导出传递因子化Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)，给出Smith数据的局部等价性与奇异性贡献，并用二阶Keldysh公式获得独立观测门，进而证明固定重置no-go定理，最后构造三重重置监测Lindblad见证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EP在开放与监测量子系统中的物理可观测性长期存在争议；有限重置监测系统中首次通过计数的EP特征能否真正体现在累积计数可观测量中，取决于谱缺陷性是否能从抽象(单记录/传递)描述传递到物理阶梯描述，以及Jordan模是否与实验制备和末态投影耦合。本文旨在澄清这一传递条件并给出严格的判定框架与反例/no-go结论。

Method: 在精确变换域(transform-domain)层面分析：对无上跳(upward-skip-free)计数的有限重置型监测量子系统，证明阈值因子化H_N(s)=R_s^N并识别物理Perron分支；导出传递因子化Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)；用余子式(cofactor)可逆性给出Smith数据的局部等价性，并处理奇异余子式导致的传递重数；利用二阶Keldysh公式构造独立观测门；证明固定重置no-go定理；构造三重重置监测Lindblad见证模型与相干调谐模型。

Result: 证明H_N(s)=R_s^N的精确阈值因子化，识别物理Perron分支并导出Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)；余子式可逆时Smith数据局部相等，奇异余子式可引入阶梯中不存在的传递重数；二阶Keldysh公式给出独立观测门；固定重置no-go定理表明即使全倾斜生成元有缺陷，当计数后阶梯为标量时也无法产生累积计数Jordan多项式；构造出在不可约非负阶梯中极小的三重重置监测Lindblad见证模型，具有真实的次领头Nλ^N阈值项，且相干调谐模型在横截变换域EP处保留精确多项式特征。

Conclusion: 首次通过可观测量的EP特征受两项独立条件支配：谱缺陷性须传递至物理单能级阶梯，且Jordan模须与制备态和末态观测有非零交叠；满足时可在变换域中保留精确多项式(EP)特征，不满足时(如固定重置下计数后阶梯为标量)则被no-go定理排除。分析限于精确变换域结构，时域渐近与微扰鲁棒性仍为待解问题。

Abstract: Exceptional-point signatures in first-passage observables are governed by two independent survival mechanisms: nonlinear spectral defectivity must pass from the one-record or transfer description to the physical one-level first-passage ladder, and the resulting ladder Jordan mode must have nonzero overlap with the chosen preparation and terminal observation. For finite reset-form monitored quantum systems with upward-skip-free counting, we prove the exact threshold factorization $H_N(s)=R_s^N$, identify the physical Perron branch, and derive the transfer factorization $Q_s(r)=B_s(r)(R_s-rI)$. Invertibility of the cofactor gives local equality of Smith data, whereas a singular cofactor can contribute transfer multiplicity absent from the ladder. An order-two Keldysh formula yields an independent observation gate. We then prove a fixed-reset no-go theorem: even a defective full tilted generator cannot generate a cumulative-count Jordan polynomial when the post-count ladder is scalar. Finally, we construct a three-reset monitored-Lindblad witness, minimal within irreducible nonnegative ladders, with a genuine subleading $Nλ^N$ threshold term and a coherence-tuned model in which a binary terminal effect retains the exact polynomial signature at a transversal transform-domain exceptional point. The analysis is formulated at the level of exact transform-domain structure; time-domain asymptotics and perturbative robustness remain separate questions.

</details>


### [76] [Rydberg quantum antennas for chip-interfaced single-photon source array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20172)
*Yan-Lei Zhang,Dong-Qi Ma,Guang-Jie Chen,Qing-Xuan Jie,Liang Chen,Ya-Dong Hu,Zhu-Bo Wang,Guang-Can Guo,Chang-Ling Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种由一维中性原子链构成的里德堡量子天线，作为芯片集成的单光子源，通过里德堡阻塞产生单一集体激发并实现定向单光子发射，模拟显示单光子收集效率超过70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子量子信息技术需要高效率、高纯度且可扩展的单光子源，并与光子芯片集成，现有方案在效率、纯度和规模化方面面临挑战。

Method: 构建由驻波光镊中一维中性原子链组成的里德堡量子天线；利用里德堡阻塞诱导原子链中的单一集体激发，借助有序几何结构将光子发射整形为定向光束；采用数值模拟评估单光子收集效率及多激发概率。

Result: 通过高数值孔径透镜，单光子进入波导的收集效率超过70%，多激发概率可忽略；该性能对驻波偶极阱中的概率性原子装载具有鲁棒性，仅需约十个原子。

Conclusion: 该里德堡量子天线为实现可扩展、高效率、高纯度且一致的单光子源阵列提供了新途径，并可集成于光子芯片，解决光子量子信息技术中的关键难题。

Abstract: We propose a Rydberg quantum antenna, consisting of a one-dimensional chain of neutral atoms trapped in a standing-wave optical tweezer, as a chip-interfaced single-photon source. Rydberg blockade induces a single collective excitation in the atomic chain, while its ordered geometry shapes the photon emission into a directional beam, thereby realizing a quantum antenna that emits strictly one photon at a time. Through numerical simulations, we demonstrate single-photon collection efficiencies into a waveguide exceeding 70\% through a high-NA lens with a negligible multiple excitation probability. These performance are robust against probabilistic atom loading in the standing-wave dipole traps, requiring only ten atoms, indicating experimental feasibility. The Rydberg quantum antenna thus offers a unique route toward scalable arrays of high-efficiency, high-purity, and identical single-photon sources interfaced with a photonic chip, addressing key challenges in photonic quantum information technology.

</details>


### [77] [QECirc: A Community-Driven, FAIR Library of Quantum Error Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20182)
*Ludwig Schmid,Tom Peham,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QECirc是一个社区驱动的开放库，按照FAIR原则整合量子纠错电路，统一提供Stim与OpenQASM格式、交互式可视化，并在发布前验证正确性，弥补了现有目录仅描述纠错码而缺少可复用电路的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错是容错量子计算的基础，但实现纠错码的电路（编码器、逻辑态制备与门操作、症状提取方案）分散在众多论文与一次性代码库中，格式互不兼容，文档稀疏，难以复现和复用。现有目录（如Error Correction Zoo）侧重于描述纠错码本身，缺少可直接使用的具体电路实现，因此需要一个统一、可复用的电路资源库。

Method: 构建并发布社区驱动、开放访问的QECirc在线库。每一条目关联其底层代码，统一以Stim和OpenQASM两种格式提供电路，并配套交互式可视化；在发布前对电路进行正确性检查，以保证可靠性与可复用性。

Result: QECirc实现了量子纠错电路的Findable、Accessible、Interoperable、Reusable（FAIR）目标，汇集了编码器、逻辑态制备与门例程、症状提取调度等具体电路，并以统一格式交付、可视化呈现、经正确性验证后发布。

Conclusion: QECirc与Error Correction Zoo形成互补：后者描述纠错码，QECirc提供实现这些码的具体电路，从而推动量子纠错电路的可查找、可访问、可互操作与可复用，促进容错量子计算研究与工程实践。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) underpins fault-tolerant quantum computing, but the circuits that realize QEC codes (encoders, logical state-preparation and gate routines, and syndrome-extraction schedules) are scattered across papers and one-off repositories in incompatible formats, often documented too sparsely to reconstruct or reuse. We present QECirc (https://qecirc.com/), a community-driven, open library that makes QEC circuits Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, and Reusable (FAIR). Each entry links to its underlying code, ships in Stim and OpenQASM with interactive visualizations, and is checked for correctness before publication. In short, where a catalog such as the Error Correction Zoo describes the codes, QECirc provides the concrete circuits that realize them.

</details>


### [78] [Fast Unitary Preparation of Surface-Code Logical States on Neutral-Atom Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20183)
*Ludwig Schmid,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免测量、幺正的表面码泡利本征态制备方案，专为中性原子平台设计，通过双向稳定子扩展CNOT级联实现，并在bloqade工具链上验证其性能优于标准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面码量子计算起始时需反复制备逻辑态。标准基于测量的协议在中性原子上代价高昂：测量速度比门操作慢数个量级，且单区架构还需将原子移动到读出区。因此需要一种免测量的幺正制备方案以适应中性原子的硬件约束。

Method: 提出双向稳定子扩展CNOT级联，从补丁中线向外扩展，深度为(d+3)/2，同时在受保护误差方向上保持故障距离d。每层对应一次集体原子移动加一次全局里德堡脉冲，约每两层拾取一行。使用开源bloqade工具链，在硬件校准的电路级中性原子噪声模型下编译并仿真所得调度序列。

Conclusion: 该免测量幺正制备方案适配中性原子的硬件特性，双向稳定子扩展级联在噪声下表现出最优逻辑错误率和阈值以下运行能力，为表面码逻辑态制备提供了高效替代路径。

Abstract: Preparing logical states is a constantly recurring primitive at the start of any surface-code-based quantum computation. In this work, we present a measurement-free, unitary preparation of surface-code Pauli eigenstates tailored to neutral atoms. A bidirectional stabilizer-expanding CNOT cascade grows the patch from its middle line outward in depth $(d{+}3)/2$, while retaining fault distance $d$ in the protected error direction. Each layer maps to one collective atom move followed by a single global Rydberg pulse, with about one row pickup per two layers. We compile and simulate the resulting schedules with the open-source bloqade toolchain under a hardware-calibrated, circuit-level neutral-atom noise model. The bidirectional cascade achieves the lowest logical error rate at every distance, and is the only construction tested that operates below threshold for both logical states at the considered device-level noise.

Abstract: Early transformer fault diagnosis is challenged by nonlinear dissolved-gas interactions, overlapping fault signatures, and limited labeled data, while practical deployment further requires reliable performance under realistic computational constraints. This paper presents a simulation-driven modeling framework for dissolved gas analysis-based transformer fault diagnosis, in which a carefully engineered variational quantum classifier (VQC) is employed as the computational core and systematically analyzed through simulation. A hybrid ZX-YY quantum feature map is designed to model non-commuting feature interactions, while a full-entanglement EfficientSU2 ansatz provides adequate expressive capacity under strict resource limits. Model behavior is evaluated using a comprehensive simulation pipeline including noise-aware circuit emulation, cross-dataset validation, and limited hardware-in-the-loop execution, allowing key effects of circuit depth, noise, and optimization strategy to be examined. Simulation results on benchmark dissolved-gas-analysis datasets demonstrate high diagnostic accuracy, strong generalization capability, and robustness to realistic noise levels with minimal quantum resources. The results highlight the effectiveness of simulation-informed modeling for practical transformer diagnostic applications, offering a reproducible and resource-efficient pathway for evaluating quantum-enhanced fault diagnosis methods.

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对委托量子计算验证协议中的"秘密独立性"假设，将其量化并指出现有高保真设备并不自动满足该假设，进而提出一种脉冲级量子控制校准框架，在不牺牲保真度的前提下强制实现秘密独立的状态制备，仿真结果显示秘密依赖性降低一个数量级以上且对常见误差鲁棒，该框架平台无关、仅在校准阶段运行。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证协议可用密码学手段保证委托量子计算结果的正确性，但此类协议依赖一个核心假设：影响态制备的噪声不得依赖于指定所制备态的经典秘密（即"秘密独立性"）。早期协议通过客户端与服务器的物理隔离来保证该假设，而在片上（on-chip）场景中，这变成硬件自身的属性，且高保真器件并不会自动满足该性质。因此有必要量化这一要求并寻找工程化实现手段。

Method: 作者首先将秘密独立性要求定量化，推导出一个界，说明残余的秘密依赖如何限制该框架内任何可验证计算的规模；随后提出一个校准框架，通过脉冲级量子控制强制实现秘密独立性，从而得到同时具备高保真度与秘密独立性的状态制备。并在氮空位（NV）色心系统上通过仿真（emulation）评估其性能。

Result: 在NV色心系统上的仿真评估表明，相比标准最优控制，该框架将制备态的秘密依赖性降低了一个数量级以上，并且对实验中常见的失谐和驱动幅度误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架完全在校准阶段运行且与平台无关，为规模化可验证量子计算提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Verification protocols provide cryptographic guarantees that the outcome of a delegated quantum computation is correct, a key requirement for scalable and trustworthy quantum computing. A central assumption underlying these protocols is secret independence: the noise affecting state preparation must not depend on the classical secrets that specify the prepared states. In earlier protocols this assumption was enforced by the physical separation of client and server; in the recent on-chip setting it becomes a property of the hardware itself, and one that high-fidelity devices do not automatically satisfy. We first make the requirement quantitative, deriving a bound that shows how residual secret dependence limits the size of any verifiable computation within this framework. Motivated by this, we introduce a calibration framework that enforces secret independence through pulse-level quantum control, producing state preparations that are simultaneously high-fidelity and secret-independent. We assess its performance on a nitrogen-vacancy centre system via emulation, showing that it reduces the secret dependence of the preparations by more than an order of magnitude compared to standard optimal control, and that it is robust against detuning and drive-amplitude errors commonly encountered in experiment. The framework operates entirely at the calibration stage and is platform-agnostic, offering a practical pathway towards verifiable quantum computing at scale.

</details>


### [81] [Phase-Flag Access and No-Go Constraints on Quotient-Space Real Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20226)
*Jeongho Bang,Kyoungho Cho,Kyunghyun Baek*

TL;DR: 将Barrios Hita等的实数量子力学商空间构造作强操作解读（实旗标为可访问自由度），会与重叠可判定性（OD）及标准no-go定理冲突：可读/可控旗标提供了OD所缺的相位约定资源，进而可实现概率克隆、基于引导的通信与对数查询的非结构搜索。故该构造要么等价于实记号下的复数量子力学，要么脱离量子操作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barrios Hita等提出实数量子力学的商空间表述，并断言复数只是便利工具。前序分析表明，当该构造与复数量子力学经验等价时，其实旗标由隐藏复结构Ĵ保护，复合规则为关于该结构的平衡张量积。本文追问更强的操作解读——即把实旗标视为可访问的实自由度——会带来什么后果，以厘清该构造的操作地位。

Method: 将实旗标强化为可读或可相干操控的操作原语，考察它与重叠可判定性（OD）及标准no-go定理的关系；论证可读/可控旗标会为每条射线固定一个代表元，从而提供相位约定；再将该资源推广为一般相位访问原语，推导其操作能力。

Result: 可读或可相干控制的旗标固定每条射线的代表元，恰好提供OD所指认的、实现未知态通用叠加所缺失的相位约定资源。一旦提升为通用相位访问原语，该资源即可实现线性相关集的概率克隆、基于引导的通信（signaling）以及对数查询复杂度的非结构搜索——均与OD和标准no-go定理直接冲突。

Conclusion: 该商空间构造有明确的后续解读：作为受保护的规范表示，它就是实记号下的标准复数量子力学；作为可访问的实理论，它则脱离量子操作框架。故复数在量子力学中并非仅是便利，实旗标的可访问性会破坏量子操作体系的核心约束。

Abstract: Barrios Hita et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 240202 (2026)] proposed a quotient-space formulation of quantum mechanics over the real numbers and concluded that complex numbers are only a convenience. A preceding analysis [arXiv:2607.05865] showed that, when the construction is empirically equivalent to complex quantum mechanics, its real flag is protected by a hidden complex structure $\hat{J}$ and its composition rule is the balanced tensor product over that structure. Here we ask what would follow from the stronger operational reading that the flag is an accessible real degree of freedom. The answer is a direct clash with overlap-determinability (OD), introduced in [arXiv:2601.14638], and with standard no-go theorems. A readable or coherently controllable flag fixes a representative of each ray, and hence supplies precisely the phase convention that OD identifies as the missing resource for universal superposition of unknown states. Once promoted to a generic phase-access primitive, this resource enables probabilistic cloning of a linearly dependent set, steering-based signaling, and logarithmic-query unstructured search. Thus the quotient construction has a sharp follow-up interpretation: as a protected gauge representation it is standard complex quantum mechanics in real notation; as an accessible real theory it leaves the quantum operational framework.

</details>

Method: 通过改变周期极化铌酸锂(periodically poled lithium niobate, PPLN)波导的温度, 在1.5 μm波段实现宽带量子光源的带宽调谐; 并在不同带宽下测量关联光子对产生率、符合偶然比(coincidence-to-accidental ratio, CAR)以及基于Franson干涉的能量-时间纠缠特性。

Conclusion: 该工作为研制带宽可调的量子光源提供了有效方法, 在大规模量子网络构建中具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum light sources constitute a crucial physical resource for the construction of quantum networks. Despite remarkable recent progress, there remains a lack of systematic investigation into the bandwidth tunability of quantum light sources under fixed waveguide parameters. In this work, we demonstrate a broadband quantum light source in the 1.5 $μ$m band with tunable bandwidth by changing the temperature of a piece of periodically poled lithium niobate waveguide. In our demonstration, the bandwidth of the quantum light source is tuned from 78.3 nm to 96.2 nm with a temperature change of 1 $^\circ$C . Under different bandwidths, the generation rates of correlated photon pairs are greater than 6.3 MHz with coincidence-to-accidental ratios consistently being no less than 608. The energy-time entanglement properties are measured by using the Franson interference with two-photon interference visibilities larger than 99.06%. Our results provide an effective method for developing the quantum light sources with tunable bandwidth which has great potential for building the large-scale quantum networks.

</details>


### [83] [Quantum Speedups for Log-Concave Sampling from Local Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20253)
*Chenghua Liu,Qisheng Wang,Zhengfeng Ji*

TL;DR: 本文研究局部查询下的对数凹采样，提出量子算法在强对数凹情形下以 Õ(√κ d) 次局部查询完成采样，优于经典 Õ(κd) 与先前量子 Õ(√κ d²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中 f(x) 常具局部结构 f(x)=Σψ_a(x_{S_a})，对每个子句的局部查询远比查询整个 f 便宜；本文旨在利用局部结构作为量子算法资源，突破经典与已有量子采样的查询复杂度。

Method: 针对每个坐标仅出现在少量子句中的强对数凹分布，设计量子采样算法，仅使用对每个子句 ψ_a(·) 的求值与梯度局部查询，查询复杂度为 Õ(√κ d)。

Result: 在对数凹采样上取得 Õ(√κ d) 的局部查询复杂度，较经典最优 Õ(κd) 与 Childs 等隐含的量子结果 Õ(√κ d²) 均有改进；适用于高斯马尔可夫随机场、有限元潜高斯模型、稀疏广义线性模型等。

Conclusion: 局部结构不仅是实现细节，而是高维采样中可利用的量子算法资源。

Abstract: For a convex function $f \colon \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$, the problem of sampling from a distribution proportional to $e^{-f(x)}$ is called log-concave sampling. In many practical scenarios, the function $f(x)$ turns out to admit a local decomposition $f(x) = \sum_{a=1}^R ψ_a(x_{S_a})$. In this paper, we consider log-concave sampling using local queries, i.e., evaluation and gradient queries to each clause $ψ_a(\cdot)$, which can be computationally much cheaper than the queries to $f(x)$ itself. We show that if each coordinate appears in only a small number of clauses, there is a quantum algorithm for strongly log-concave sampling using $\widetilde{O}(\sqrtκd)$ local queries, where $κ$ is the condition number. This improves the prior best classical result $\widetilde{O}(κd)$ due to Ascolani, Lavenant, and Zanella (Ann. Probab. 2026) and the quantum result $\widetilde{O}(\sqrtκ d^2)$ implied by Childs et al. (NeurIPS 2022). Our quantum sampler applies to a broad class of locally structured models from statistical computing and machine learning, with representative examples including Gaussian Markov random fields, finite-element latent Gaussian models, and sparse generalized linear models. These results demonstrate that local structure is not merely an implementation detail, but a quantum algorithmic resource for high-dimensional sampling.

</details>

Method: 将信号在笛卡尔坐标下表述，从而规避弱信号情形下的奇异性，建立规整的多参数量子估计框架；在该框架下优化量子传感器网络，并与贝叶斯推断结合，重建轴子-光子耦合。系统采用纠缠超导量子比特构成量子传感器网络进行量子色动力学（QCD）轴子暗物质的直接探测。

Conclusion: 通过笛卡尔坐标表述规避弱信号奇异性，将轴子探测建模为双参数量子估计问题，并借助优化的量子传感器网络与贝叶斯推断，可实现对QCD轴子暗物质的高精度、鲁棒探测，适用于基准耦合与扩展质量范围。

Abstract: We propose a quantum sensor network for direct detection of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion dark matter using entangled superconducting qubits. In the presence of a static magnetic field, the oscillating axion field induces a coherent qubit rotation described by an encoding angle proportional to the axion-photon coupling and an unknown dark-matter phase. Treating the phase as an additional unknown parameter turns axion detection into a two-parameter quantum estimation problem. We formulate these signals in Cartesian coordinates, thereby avoiding the singularity that arises in the weak-signal regime and establishing a regular framework for multiparameter quantum estimation. Using this framework, we optimize the quantum sensor network and combine it with Bayesian inference to reconstruct the axion-photon coupling. The optimized protocol accurately recovers the KSVZ and DFSZ benchmark couplings over the mass range $m_a\in[0.1,10]~μ{\rm eV}$, generalizes to previously unseen masses, and remains robust against realistic superconducting-qubit decoherence and gate errors.

</details>


### [85] [A Protocol-Guided LLM Agent for Quantum Program Synthesis and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20258)
*Ming-Kang Ho,Tai-Yue Li*

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种协议引导的大语言模型智能体工作流，用于量子程序合成与执行，证明协议约束能显著提升可靠性，但仍需辅以量子语义验证。

Conclusion: 协议引导可作为一种软件可靠性层，但必须与量子语义验证结合，以区分评测器完成与语义有效性。

Abstract: This study evaluates a protocol-guided large language model (LLM) agent workflow for quantum program synthesis and execution. A versioned YAML protocol specifies interface and quantum-semantic requirements while leaving circuit design to the model. The workflow combines Qiskit circuit generation, evaluator-guided repair, and quantum processing unit (QPU) deployment. A matched ablation evaluates four LLM endpoints using the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) for H$_2$, the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for MaxCut, and Fashion-MNIST quantum-kernel classification. Across 240 trials, protocol guidance increased evaluator completion from 80.0\% to 99.2\%, reduced mean repairs from 2.56 to 0.75, and reduced mean agent time from 55.70 to 30.58~s. Provider-reported token use decreased for three of four endpoints. Completed VQE trials meeting chemical accuracy increased from 16/40 to 29/40, while valid QAOA approximation ratios remained comparable. Semantic review identified rank-one fidelity kernels, reinforcing the distinction between evaluator completion and semantic validity. A 210-run IBM QPU study evaluated the combined generation-and-selection pipelines and confirmed deployment feasibility. The results support protocol guidance as a software-reliability layer that must be complemented by quantum-semantic validation.

</details>


### [86] [QEncodeBench: Can Large Language Models Encode Classical Problems into Verified Quantum Oracles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20319)
*Xujun Che,Hanhan Wu,Yuchen Yuan,Chenyang Yu*

TL;DR: 论文提出QEncodeBench，将"经典约束问题编码为量子相位预言机"这一被文献默认的前提转化为可测量能力，用对抗式自验证验证器评测LLM生成电路，发现采样基态测试会高估能力，推理模式显著提升准确率，失败多属语义而非语法，两种架构可弥补差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover搜索、振幅放大与量子计数均依赖同一可复用子程序——相位预言机，而现有算法文献默认经典谓词已被正确编码为资源受限电路，未将"编码"本身作为能力加以度量。论文旨在填补这一空白，评估LLM能否胜任该编码任务。

Method: 构建QEncodeBench基准，让LLM将经典约束问题编码为相位预言机；采用对抗式自验证验证器，在忽略全局相位意义下判定解集完全等价，并要求辅助比特复原与资源预算约束；对比采样基态测试，并设计单元验证约束智能体与神经符号编译流水线两种架构，辅以消融实验与难度递进实验。

Result: 采样基态测试会系统性高估模型能力；无推理模式的代码模型几乎无法求解，同一权重下启用原生推理可将准确率提升一个数量级；失败主要源于语义而非语法；两种架构通过将正确性关键的组合交由确定性过程完成，弥补了大部分剩余差距；消融量化了各组件的贡献，资源门控揭示了依赖架构的宽度-深度权衡；不同难度轴对不同方法造成差异化退化，而神经符号流水线通过了所有评测实例。

Conclusion: 将相位预言机构建形式化为可测量基准是可行且必要的；LLM的量子编码能力需在严格等价性验证下评估，采样测试不可靠；原生推理与符号化/确定性架构是提升正确性的关键方向。

Abstract: Grover search, amplitude amplification, and quantum counting all rely on the same reusable subroutine, a phase oracle, whose construction the algorithms literature takes as given: the classical predicate is assumed to be already encoded as a correct, resource-bounded circuit. We turn this assumption into a measured capability. QEncodeBench tasks large language models (LLMs) with encoding classical constraint problems as phase oracles and scores the generated circuits with an adversarially self-validated verifier that decides full solution-set equivalence up to a global phase, with ancillas restored and resource budgets enforced. Sampled basis-state tests, we show, systematically overestimate this ability. Measured this way, models separate sharply: code models without a reasoning mode solve essentially nothing, and enabling native reasoning on identical weights improves accuracy by an order of magnitude. The failures are overwhelmingly semantic rather than syntactic. Two architectures, a unit-verified constraint agent and a neuro-symbolic compilation pipeline, close most of the remaining gap by delegating correctness-critical composition to deterministic procedures. Ablations quantify the contribution of each component, and resource gating exposes an architecture-dependent trade-off between circuit width and depth. Finally, controlled difficulty escalation reveals architecture-specific responses to difficulty structure: different difficulty axes degrade different methods, while the neuro-symbolic pipeline passes every evaluated instance. Code and data are available at https://github.com/chexujun/QEncodeBench.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算性能增强此前多与动力学相变相关联，但该关联是否适用于耗散系统、以及其背后的弛豫机制尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对Liouvillian弛豫谱与计算容量之间直接、定量的联系。

Method: 构建驱动耗散Kerr储层模型，系统比较一阶与连续耗散相变两类情形下的计算性能（记忆容量与非线性处理）；在热力学极限下考察Liouvillian能隙闭合与相变的关系；并对训练后的储层进行精确的Liouvillian模式分解，将训练所得记忆定量归因于各内在弛豫通道，分析能隙模式、有限速率模式及其交叉贡献。

Result: 在一阶与连续耗散相变的相边界附近均观察到记忆与非线性处理能力的增强；尽管Liouvillian能隙闭合在热力学极限下标志相变，但计算性能总体上并不随能隙抑制而变化；模式分解显示能隙模式贡献较弱，而有限速率模式及其交叉贡献主导增强的计算容量。

Conclusion: 研究超越唯象关联，将记忆容量直接与内在Liouvillian弛豫谱联系起来，为耗散量子储层的设计以及可在实验上实现的Kerr平台上检验记忆增强机制提供了物理依据。

Abstract: Enhanced performance of quantum reservoir computing has been associated with dynamical phase transitions, but whether this connection extends to dissipative systems and which relaxation mechanisms underlie it remain insufficiently understood. We systematically compare driven-dissipative Kerr reservoirs across first-order and continuous dissipative phase transitions and find enhanced memory and nonlinear processing near both phase boundaries. Although the closure of the Liouvillian gap marks the transitions in the thermodynamic limit, computational performance does not generally follow gap suppression. An exact post-training Liouvillian-mode decomposition quantitatively attributes the trained memory to intrinsic relaxation channels. It shows that the gap mode contributes only weakly, while finite-rate modes and their cross-contributions dominate the enhanced capacity. These results go beyond phenomenological correlations by directly linking memory capacity to the intrinsic Liouvillian relaxation spectrum. Moreover, these findings provide a physical basis for designing dissipative quantum reservoirs and testing memory-enhancement mechanisms in experimentally accessible Kerr platforms.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究由网络Katz核驱动的非局域Ising相互作用下量子态的纠缠动力学，推导出纠缠距离的精确解析表达式，并在不同传播区域（弱Katz/强传播）刻画其结构，将其与网络的游走与谱结构联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠动力学通常依赖于底层相互作用网络的结构，但将其与复杂网络的游走（walk-based）性质和谱性质定量联系的研究仍不足。本文以Katz核作为非局域Ising耦合，动机来自同一图上传播的带隙费米子媒介，通过微扰消除自然导出Katz加权的有效Ising哈密顿量，从而建立一个连接纠缠与网络结构的物理可解释框架。

Method: 在图上定义Katz核非局域Ising模型，由图上带隙费米子媒介的微扰消除导出有效哈密顿量；使用纠缠距离（Entanglement Distance）度量初始可分态的纠缠生成，推导精确解析表达式并应用于确定性图族；在弱Katz区域做系统性的motif展开（一阶出现三角形，二阶出现四环、局部度结构和重叠三角形），在强传播区域用邻接矩阵主模及其本征向量局域性刻画；对Erdős–Rényi图对弱传播展开做解析平均。

Result: 得到初始可分态的纠缠距离的精确解析表达式并应用于代表性确定性图族；弱Katz区纠缠动力学具有motif展开结构，三角形进入一阶、四环/局部度/重叠三角形进入二阶；强传播区动力学由邻接矩阵主模及本征向量局域性主导；Erdős–Rényi图上解析平均显示稀疏区呈现局部树状贡献，稠密区纠缠距离密度饱和。

Conclusion: 纠缠动力学可由网络的游走结构与谱结构同时决定，Katz核Ising模型提供了将纠缠生成、motif展开和谱主模、以及随机图（稀疏局部树状与稠密饱和）统一起来的解析框架。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement dynamics of quantum states defined on graphs with non-local Ising interactions governed by the Katz kernel of the underlying network. The interaction pattern is physically motivated by a gapped fermionic mediator propagating on the same graph, whose perturbative elimination yields an effective Katz-weighted Ising Hamiltonian. Using the Entanglement Distance, we derive an exact analytical expression for the entanglement generated from an initially separable state and apply it to representative deterministic graph families.
  We then characterize the dynamics in different propagation regimes. In the weak-Katz regime, the dynamics admits a systematic motif expansion with triangles entering at first order order and four-cycles, local degree structure, and overlappin triangles appearing at second order. In the strong-propagation regime, the interaction is instead dominated by the principal adjacency mode and by the localization properties of its eigenvector. For Erdős--Rényi graphs, the weak-propagation expansion can be averaged analytically, revealing a locally tree-like contribution in the sparse regime and saturation of the Entanglement Distance density in the dense regime.
  Our results connect entanglement dynamics with both the walk-based and spectral structure of complex networks.

</details>


### [89] [The effects of shot noise on the quantum computation of NMR spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20406)
*Sebastian Walch,Keith R. Fratus,Jan-Michael Reiner,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以核磁共振(NMR)谱模拟为测试案例，研究量子模拟中投影测量所需采样次数（散粒噪声）如何随系统规模扩展，发现资源需求仅随分子尺寸弱增长，远低于希尔伯特空间的指数增长，表明散粒噪声不构成实现量子优势的根本障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算硬件近期进展促使人们寻找能体现"量子优势"的应用，量子多体系统模拟是候选之一。经典计算机模拟资源随系统规模指数增长（源于希尔伯特空间指数增长），数字量子计算机虽天然在该空间中演化量子态，却无法直接给出确定性输出，而需依赖投影测量，受散粒噪声影响，须通过重复测量重构期望值。因此测量次数成为关键计算资源，直接关系到量子优势论断能否成立，需要明确其随规模的扩展规律。

Method: 以NMR谱模拟为测试案例，同时研究真实分子和一类模型NMR哈密顿量（支持一维、二维及全连接相互作用，便于高效大尺度模拟），分析所需测量次数随系统规模及目标精度变化的标度关系。

Result: 所需资源（测量次数）仅随分子尺寸弱增长，远低于底层希尔伯特空间的指数增长；该标度行为在真实分子与模型哈密顿量中均成立。

Conclusion: 散粒噪声不应被视为在NMR系统模拟中实现量子优势的根本障碍，对于更广泛的多体系统而言可能同样如此。

Abstract: Recent advances in the field of quantum computing hardware motivate the search for applications which demonstrate so-called quantum advantage. One promising use case that has been identified is the simulation of quantum many-body systems. The computational resources required for performing such a simulation on a classical computer generally grow exponentially with the size of the system being modeled, which is ultimately due to the exponential growth of the Hilbert space in which the dynamics of such a system take place. While digital quantum computers natively evolve quantum states directly in such a Hilbert space, thus naively avoiding this problem, the result of such a computation is typically not obtained as a deterministic output. Rather, it requires projective measurements which are fundamentally affected by shot noise. Any desired expectation values must therefore be reconstructed from repeated measurements, making the number of those measurements a relevant computational resource, and thus an important consideration for any potential claims of quantum advantage. In this work, we study how this resource scales with system size (a scaling which itself depends on the desired accuracy of the final result), using the simulation of nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra as a test case. We study this scaling for both real-world molecules, as well as a class of model NMR Hamiltonians which allow for efficient large-scale simulations with one-dimensional, two-dimensional, and all-to-all interactions. We find that the required resources increase only weakly with molecular size, far below the exponential growth of the underlying Hilbert space. This result suggests that shot noise should not pose a fundamental barrier to achieving quantum advantage in the simulation of NMR systems, and perhaps for many-body systems more broadly.

</details>


### [90] [State-Selective Floquet Memory in Degenerate Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20428)
*Ayan Sahoo,Argha Debnath,Debraj Rakshit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究周期驱动下近简并流形中的记忆保留问题，指出简并本身不决定性，关键在于扰动在投影多重态上的作用，并给出两条稳定性判据，在交替XXZ链上验证了长寿命态与边带共振等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想极限下不变结构（如Floquet/记忆保持态）的研究通常依赖理想化条件，但实际驱动与结构存在不完美。为理解哪些初态在周期驱动下真正保留记忆，必须离开该理想极限，进入近简并流形中分析，考察微扰如何作用于投影后的多重态。

Method: 采用简并微扰论，对理想极限下解耦为局部团簇的系统计算投影。给出两条稳定性判据：(1) 耦合不能将目标态与流形内其他简并伙伴相连——可通过动理学阻塞（kinetic blocking），或更一般地通过对投影耦合做对角化来实现；(2) 驱动的局域选择定则只允许转移能量偏离Floquet边带（detuned from Floquet sidebands），此时投影的驱动生成元精确为零。随后在周期驱动的干净、短程交替XXZ链中形式化上述理解。

Result: 在交替XXZ链中展示了一系列结果：长寿命态，包括乘积形式与跨团簇纠缠形式的态；一个周期性的边带共振族；以及一个明确示例，表明能量与电荷相同的态可以有相反的命运（fate）。

Conclusion: 简并本身不足以决定记忆保留，真正决定稳定性的是微扰（结构与驱动不完美）在投影多重态上的具体作用。两条判据——投影耦合的不可连接性与驱动局域选择定则的边带失谐——共同决定了长寿命态的存在。该框架可解释并预测周期驱动系统中保持记忆的初态集合。

Abstract: In order to understand which initial states retain memory under periodic driving, it is necessary to depart from the ideal limit giving rise to invariant structures and work inside near-degenerate manifolds. We show that degeneracy itself is not conclusive and what actually matters is how perturbations, such as structural and driving imperfections, acts on the projected multiplet. For systems decoupling into the local clusters in the ideal limit, the projections can be computed within degenerate perturbation theory. There the stability is decided by two criteria: the coupling can not connect the state with other degenerate partners in the manifold, either by kinetic blocking, or more generally, by diagonalizing the projected coupling, and the drive's local selection rule must admit only transfer energies detuned from Floquet sidebands, in which case the projected drive generator exactly vanishes. We formalize these understandings in a periodically driven clean, short-ranged alternating XXZ chain and demonstrate a set of results that include, long-lived states, both of product form and entangled across the clusters, a periodic family of sideband resonances, and an explicit example showing opposite fates for states with identical energy and charges.

</details>

*Lucia Malickova*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实化学体系上验证变分量子特征求解器（VQE）流程，是迈向经典不可解分子的关键一步；本文针对铜活性位点，旨在为后续物理量子硬件运行建立可靠的模拟基线。

Abstract: Validating variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) pipelines on realistic chemical systems is a critical stepping stone toward tackling classically intractable molecules. In this work, we report emulator-based validation of the VQE workflow proposed for the copper active site, executed on the LRZ Eviden Qaptiva emulator. A problem-tailored ADAPT-VQE with a full singles-doubles-triples (S+D+T) pool removes over 99% of the correlation error, reaching a stable plateau at 4.03 mEh relative to exact diagonalisation (148 selected operators). This establishes a >40x improvement over the hardware-efficient ansatz (HEA). Finally, we frame these emulator results as a robust baseline for upcoming physical quantum hardware runs on Euro-Q-Exa.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建Transformer-based Quantum State Characterizer（TQSC）模型，从含噪测量中重建实验制备的纯态与混合态光子偏振态；利用注意力模式揭示测量可观测量间的关联；并用Qiskit模拟Bloch球态检验鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Quantum communication underpins secure information processing and scalable quantum networks. In particular, remote state preparation (RSP) enables efficient quantum state transfer, but accurately estimating target states under complex noise remains challenging. Here, we propose a Transformer-based Quantum State Characterizer (TQSC) model for noisy RSP experiments. Our model reconstructs experimentally prepared pure and mixed photonic polarization states from noisy measurements in complex scattering environments, while its attention patterns provide physically grounded insights into correlations among the measured observables. The method achieves a mean estimator-target fidelity exceeding 99.999% under complex scattering and dynamic Gaussian noise, while its robustness and generalization are further examined using Qiskit-simulated Bloch-ball states.Furthermore, in a practical MNIST image transmission task with held-out states, the decoded bit error rate is reduced from 50.34% to zero after TQSC post-processing. The TQSC model enables accurate tomographic characterization under dynamic noise and provides physically grounded post-hoc insights, holding promise for intelligent quantum information processing applications.
