<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC 码对随机 Pauli 噪声有非零阈值的优势已被广泛认识，但相干误差本质不同——它产生误差模式的叠加，其振幅在综合征测量后仍可干涉。现有理论对相干误差的严格理解十分有限，缺乏一般性鲁棒性保证，这是容错理论中长期存在的挑战。

Method: 作者针对任意 qLDPC 码族，在局部相干噪声及局部信道噪声模型下建立码容量阈值证明。关键技术是"簇重求和"（cluster resummation）：不在信道展开中逐个界定叠加的误差构型，而是先孤立出一个大的连通误差簇，将所有与该簇不连通的误差精确重求和后再取范数；随后用标准簇计数（cluster counting）论证得到指数抑制。该方法同时适用于最优恢复与最小权重解码器。

Result: 对任意满足 d=Ω(log n) 的 qLDPC 码族，存在常数噪声强度阈值，使逻辑恢复误差以钻石距离度量随码距指数衰减；该结论对最优恢复和最小权重解码器均成立。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了容错理论中长期存在的难题，为 qLDPC 码对抗相干噪声提供了通用鲁棒性保证，并为未来容错量子技术的研究奠定了严格理论基础。

Abstract: A key appeal of quantum low-density parity check (qLDPC) codes is their ability to suppress stochastic Pauli noise below nonzero thresholds. Coherent errors are fundamentally different: they produce superpositions of error patterns whose amplitudes can interfere even after syndrome measurement. Rigorous understanding of coherent errors remains limited. Here we show that general qLDPC codes admit a nonzero code capacity threshold against local coherent noise and more generally local channel noise. For any family of qLDPC codes with distance $d=Ω(\log n)$, we show that there is a constant noise strength below which the logical recovery error in diamond distance decays exponentially with the code distance. The result is established for optimal recovery as well as the minimum-weight decoder. The key technical ingredient is what we call a \emph{cluster resummation}: rather than bounding superposed error configurations one by one, we isolate a large connected error cluster in the channel expansion and exactly resum all errors disconnected from it before taking norms. Standard cluster counting then yields exponential suppression. This work resolves a longstanding challenge in fault tolerance theory, providing general robustness guarantees for qLDPC codes against coherent noise and laying a rigorous foundation for future studies of fault-tolerant quantum technologies.

</details>


### [95] [Technical Report OFDM-Assisted Simultaneous Quantum and Classical THz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20542)
*Xin Liu,Chao Xu,Soon Xin Ng,Mohammed El-Hajjar,Phuc V. Trinh,Shinya Sugiura,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在光学与太赫兹（THz）频段通过叠加相干态同时传输量子密钥与经典信息的低成本同步量子-经典通信（SQCC）方案的可行性，针对现实频率选择性多径THz衰落信道，提出基于OFDM的SQCC系统，并以LDPC编码的多维QKD协商方案支撑，仿真表明其在宽功率分配范围内可取得实用密钥率（SKR），而单载波方案在THz SQCC中要求经典信号比量子信号强至少100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有THz SQCC方案假设平坦衰落信道下的单载波（SC）传输，未考虑真实频率选择性多径THz衰落信道，导致方案在现实场景中的可行性存疑；同时需要兼顾成本效益与量子、经典信号的同时传输。

Method: 在光学与THz频段考察叠加相干态实现SQCC的可行性；针对时间不变频率选择性THz信道，提出基于正交频分复用（OFDM）的SQCC系统，并采用低密度奇偶校验码（LDPC）编码的多维QKD协商方案作为支撑，与单载波方案进行对比仿真。

Result: 仿真结果显示，基于OFDM的SQCC方案在经典信号与量子信号之间广泛的功率分配场景下均能实现实用的密钥率（SKR）；相比之下，其单载波对应方案在THz SQCC中要求经典信号功率至少为量子信号的100倍。

Conclusion: OFDM-based SQCC在频率选择性多径THz信道中显著优于单载波方案，降低了对经典信号功率占优的要求，为THz频段同步量子-经典通信的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The feasibility of cost-effective simultaneous quantum and classical communication (SQCC) transmitting both the quantum key and classical information via a superimposed coherent state is investigated both in optical and Terahertz (THz) bands. Since the existing THz SQCC schemes assume single-carrier (SC) transmission over flat fading channels, we embark on investigating SQCC in realistic frequency-selective multipath THz fading channels. We then propose an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based SQCC system for time-invariant frequency-selective THz scenarios, supported by low-density parity-check coded (LDPC) multidimensional QKD reconciliation schemes. Our simulation results demonstrate that the OFDM-based SQCC scheme is capable of achieving a practical secret key rate (SKR) over a wide range of power sharing scenarios between the classical and quantum signals. By contrast its single-carrier counterpart requires the classical signal to be at least 100 times stronger than the quantum signal in THz SQCC.

</details>


### [96] [Scalable logical qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20549)
*Matthias Troyer,Chetan Nayak,John Martinis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出"可扩展逻辑量子比特"的定义与四个耦合维度（可靠性、规模、能力、性能），以统一衡量和比较逻辑量子比特的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用级量子计算需要执行长而复杂的算法，端到端错误率需远低于物理量子比特可直接支持的水平，因此必须使用纠错后的逻辑量子比特。然而，如何使逻辑量子比特的进展可测量、可比较尚缺乏清晰标准。

Method: 作者提出"可扩展逻辑量子比特"的定义：通过重复量子纠错在长计算中保持的逻辑量子比特，能够以低延迟实时解码与反馈支持容错通用操作，并可复制到数百或数千个以满足应用需求。随后沿可靠性、规模、能力、性能四个耦合维度对其进行刻画，并讨论这些维度间的权衡。

Result: 给出了可扩展逻辑量子比特的概念框架及四个耦合维度（可靠性、规模、能力、性能）的刻画方式，并阐明维度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过引入可扩展逻辑量子比特的定义与多维度表征，为逻辑量子比特的进展提供了可测量、可比较的评估标准，有助于推动实用级量子计算的发展。

Abstract: Utility-scale quantum computing will require executing long, complex algorithms with end-to-end error rates far below what physical qubits can support directly. Error-corrected logical qubits are needed to achieve this goal. To make the progress on logical qubits measurable and comparable we introduce the definition of scalable logical qubits: logical qubits preserved for long computations by repeated quantum error correction, capable of fault-tolerant universal operations with low-latency real-time decoding and feedback, and replicable to the hundreds or thousands, as required by applications. We characterize these scalable logical qubits along four coupled dimensions: reliability, scale, capability, and performance, and discuss trade-offs among these dimensions.

</details>


### [97] [FT-Weave: Real-Time Compilation Framework for Reconfigurable Fault-Tolerant Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20573)
*Wan-Hsuan Lin,Milan Kornjača,Chen Zhao,Sheng-Tao Wang,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 FT-Weave，一个阶段感知的实时容错量子计算编译框架，联合协调资源制备、分配、路由与纠错处理，在两个中性原子早期 FTQC 架构上实现最高 3 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模容错量子计算（FTQC）需要在严格的硬件与时间约束下协调资源制备、分配、路由和逻辑执行。现有编译方法多采用离线调度，基于标称或固定的魔态工厂吞吐量，无法响应随机的资源制备与传态结果，导致执行停顿和硬件利用率不足。

Method: 提出 FT-Weave 阶段感知实时编译框架，联合协调资源制备、资源分配、传态路由与纠错处理；通过适应运行时资源可用性与硬件约束，使制备、通信和逻辑执行相互重叠。在两种代表性中性原子早期 FTQC 架构（transversal STAR 和 T-state cultivation）上实例化。

Result: 在评估的硬件与延迟模型下，针对二维横向场 Ising 模型仿真，FT-Weave 相比基线编译流程实现最高 3 倍加速。案例研究还发现，最大化暴露的并发性并不一定能最小化执行时间：细粒度异步执行虽可减少局部空闲时间，但其更小的优化窗口和更高的路由竞争可能抵消这些收益。

Conclusion: 有效的运行时协调，而非单纯暴露并行性，决定了 FTQC 资源转化为应用吞吐量的效率；FT-Weave 为跨资源协议与架构解决此类实时编排问题提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) is essential for large-scale quantum computation, but realizing useful application throughput requires coordinating resource preparation, assignment, routing, and logical execution under strict hardware and timing constraints. Many FTQC compilation approaches construct offline schedules using nominal or fixed magic-state factory throughput. Such schedules cannot respond to stochastic resource-preparation and teleportation outcomes, leading to execution stalls and hardware underutilization. In this work, we introduce FT-Weave, a stage-aware real-time FTQC compilation framework that jointly coordinates resource preparation, resource assignment, teleportation routing, and correction handling. By adapting to runtime resource availability and hardware constraints, FT-Weave allows preparation, communication, and logical execution to overlap. We instantiate FT-Weave on two representative neutral-atom, early FTQC architectures: transversal STAR and a T-state cultivation architecture. Under the evaluated hardware and latency model, FT-Weave achieves a speedup of up to 3X over a baseline compilation flow for simulations of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model. In the case study, we further find that maximizing exposed concurrency does not necessarily minimize execution time. Although fine-grained asynchronous execution can reduce local idle time, its smaller optimization windows and increased routing contention can outweigh these gains. Together, these results show that effective runtime coordination, rather then exposed parallelism alone, determines how efficiently FTQC resources translate into application throughput:FT-Weave provides a blueprint for solving this real-time orchestration problem across resource protocols and architectures.

</details>


### [98] [Experimental demonstration of finite-size general security via discrete-modulated CVQKD with real time postprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20596)
*Sven Bodenstedt,Carlos Pascual-García,Nil Canta i Pujol,Martí Sales-Moragues,Mariana Navarro,Pau Gómez Kabelka,Sebastián Etcheverry,Saeed Ghasemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了离散调制连续变量量子密钥分发（DM CVQKD）系统，基于QPSK方案，在约10^6轮有限码长下针对一般攻击生成可组合安全密钥，采用变长一般安全框架，结合熵累积技术与锥优化，并能在近商用硬件上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVQKD与电信基础设施兼容，但以实验可行的资源实现可组合安全性一直困难，对离散调制（DM）协议尤为如此。现有工作缺乏在有限码长下针对一般攻击的可组合安全密钥的实验验证。

Method: 采用QPSK调制的DM CVQKD实验系统；实现变长、一般安全框架，该框架由现代熵累积技术与锥优化（conic optimization）支撑；构建可实时运行的实验流水线，部署于近商用硬件。

Result: 首次实验证明DM CVQKD系统可在约10^6轮有限码长块下，针对一般攻击生成可组合安全密钥；系统在近商用硬件上实现实时运行。

Conclusion: 该工作将DM CVQKD从理论可组合安全推进到实验可实现的层面，为实用化、高安全等级的连续变量量子密钥分发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CVQKD) is compatible with telecommunication infrastructure, but implementing composable security with experimentally practical resources has remained challenging, particularly for discrete-modulated (DM) protocols. We report the first experimental demonstration of a DM CVQKD system that generates composable secret keys against general attacks with finite-size block lengths on the order of $\sim 10^{6}$ rounds via a quadrature phase shift keying (QPSK) system. Our implementation follows a variable-length, general security framework enabled by modern entropy accumulation techniques and conic optimization, whose experimental pipeline allows real-time operation on near-commercial hardware.

</details>


### [99] [Proof of Shor's conjecture on the accessible information of quantum dichotomies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20600)
*Michele Dall'Arno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文彻底解决了Shor约三十年前提出的猜想：任意量子二分量系综（两个态的系综）的可及信息由投影测量达到。作者在任意维度上给出了自包含的基本证明，并构造了优于任意测量的显式投影测量，同时将二分量系综可及信息的计算表述为凸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可及信息量化了从量子系综中可提取的最大Shannon信息量。Shor约三十年前猜想：任意量子二分量系综的可及信息由投影测量达到。此前仅有Keil针对量子比特情形给出证明，该长期公开问题在任意维度上尚未解决。

Method: 第一，证明Shor猜想在任意维度上可由Fang、Fawzi和Fawzi的近期结果推出，并提供自包含的基本证明；第二，对任意给定的二分量系综和任意测量，显式构造出在该系综信息提取上优于该测量的投影测量；第三，将二分量系综可及信息的计算表述为凸问题。

Result: 确立了Shor猜想在任意维度下成立；给出了优于任意测量的显式投影测量构造；将一般情形下非凸的可及信息计算，针对二分量系综转化为凸问题。

Conclusion: Shor关于二分量系综可及信息由投影测量达到的猜想被彻底解决，且该问题的计算可归结为凸优化，为量子系综信息提取提供了完整的理论刻画与可计算框架。

Abstract: The accessible information of any given quantum ensemble quantifies the maximum amount of Shannon information that can be extracted from the ensemble by any quantum measurement. Almost three decades ago, Shor conjectured that the accessible information of any quantum dichotomy, that is, an ensemble of two states, is attained by a projective measurement. Recently, a proof of this conjecture restricted to the qubit case was published by Keil. Here, we conclusively settle this longstanding open problem. First, we show that Shor's conjecture follows, in arbitrary dimension, from a recent result by Fang, Fawzi and Fawzi, and we provide a self-contained, elementary proof. Second, for any given dichotomy and measurement, we provide the explicit construction of a projective measurement that outperforms such a measurement in extracting information from the given dichotomy. Third, while the computation of the accessible information is known to be non-convex in general, we frame the computation of the accessible information of dihotomies as a convex problem.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum Noise Limited Nonlinear Phase-Preserving Amplification of a Bosonic Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20644)
*Abel te Riele,Tzula B. Propp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子力学允许同时满足相位保持与场算符非线性的确定性放大方案，并对这类噪声受限的非线性相位保持放大器进行了完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献研究的是更一般的噪声受限非线性放大器，但其是否能在所有玻色相位空间角度下均匀工作（即相位保持）尚不明确；本文旨在回答相位保持的确定性非线性放大是否存在及其在量子信息中的用途。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究受相位保持条件约束的噪声受限非线性放大器，给出其形式上的强约束，从而刻画整个放大器类别。

Result: 证明这类放大器确实存在，但其形式比一般的噪声受限非线性放大器受到更强约束；据此完整刻画了所有玻色噪声受限非线性相位保持放大器。

Conclusion: 该类放大器可用于量子信息科学，具体体现为对Yurke-Stoler猫态和Kerr小猫态的最优放大。

Abstract: Does quantum mechanics allow for deterministic amplification schemes that simultaneously behave uniformly for all angles of bosonic phase space (i.e. phase preserving) and are nonlinear in the underlying field operators? Would such a scheme have some utility to quantum information science? In this paper we answer both questions with a resounding yes. Such amplifiers exist, but their form is highly constrained compared to the more general set of noise limited nonlinear amplifiers previously studied in the literature. This enables us to characterize the entire class of bosonic noise-limited nonlinear phase preserving amplifiers, and identify their utility: namely, the optimal amplification of Yurke-Stoler cat states and Kerr kitten states.

</details>


### [101] [Hardness of Pathfinding in a Welded Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20651)
*David Miloschewsky,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在焊接树上从入口到出口寻找路径，任意量子查询算法至少需要 Ω(2^{n/24}) 次查询，即该任务不存在高效量子算法，从而回答了量子游走虽能指数加速定位出口、却无法高效构造路径这一开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子游走可在焊接树上以指数级快于经典算法的方式找到出口顶点，但"能否高效地找出一条从入口到出口的路径"长期未决。该问题区分了"定位目标顶点"与"构造目标路径"两类任务的量子可计算性边界，具有重要理论意义。

Method: 采用压缩置换预言机（compressed permutation oracle）技术，构造用于记录算法已学习与已遗忘的图信息的数据库，并据此论证任何高效量子算法都无法在这些记录中构建出入口到出口的路径；进而导出量子查询下界。

Result: 对高度为 n 的树，任何量子查询算法若要以常数概率成功找到入口到出口的路径，至少需要 Ω(2^{n/24}) 次查询，即该任务存在指数级量子查询下界。

Conclusion: 在焊接树上寻找入口到出口路径不存在高效量子算法；这揭示了量子游走在焊接树问题上的加速能力仅限于找到出口顶点，而不能扩展到显式构造路径，澄清了该类问题的量子算法能力边界。

Abstract: Starting from the entrance of a welded tree, a quantum walk algorithm can find its exit vertex exponentially faster than any classical algorithm. However, it has been an open question whether any quantum algorithm is able to efficiently find a path from the entrance to the exit. We answer this by proving an exponential quantum query lower bound for finding such path. Specifically, for trees of height $n$, any quantum query algorithm requires at least $Ω(2^{n/24})$ queries in order to succeed with constant probability.
  Our proof uses the compressed permutation oracle technique in order to construct databases which track the graph information an algorithm has learned and forgotten, and show that no efficient quantum algorithm can build an entrance-to-exit path in these records.

</details>


### [102] [Multinegativity and single-letter formulas for asymptotic entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20698)
*Raphael Brinster,Tulja Varun Kondra,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入一个可计算上界递减层级来刻画关于PPT态的渐近相对熵纠缠，并据此建立态依赖的单字母公式，同时证伪了纠缠代价层级的有限坍缩猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠研究常需在无穷多份态上评估度量（正则化），而单字母公式虽能将渐近纠缠量化为单份态计算，却极少存在。作者希望找到可计算且能刻画渐近相对熵纠缠的上界结构。

Method: 构造一个关于PPT态渐近相对熵纠缠的可计算上界递减层级；每一固定层级的正则化等于该渐近量。进一步把上界构造推广到夹层Rényi散度，并提出k-多重负态（binegative态的推广）与k-多重负性概念。

Result: 第一非平凡层级在两个含所有Werner态的多参数族上具有可加性，从而给出单字母公式，即使通常的单份相对熵不可加。构造出在任意大深度k上k-多重负的态，分离了先前纠缠代价层级的相邻层级，证伪其有限坍缩猜想，并给出渐近相对熵与精确PPT纠缠代价的显式上界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了态依赖的单字母公式，同时揭示了通用有限层级刻画的内在局限。

Abstract: The study of entanglement inevitably leads to the concept of regularization, where measures are evaluated on infinitely many copies of a state. Single-letter formulas try to make these asymptotic entanglement quantities accessible through a calculation on one copy of a state, but are rarely available. We introduce a decreasing hierarchy of computable upper bounds on the asymptotic relative entropy of entanglement with respect to positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The regularization of every fixed hierarchy level equals this asymptotic quantity. Additivity at any level therefore yields a single-letter formula, even when the usual one-copy relative entropy is nonadditive. We establish such additivity at the first nontrivial level for two broad multiparameter families, both containing all Werner states. The upper-bound construction also extends to sandwiched Renyi divergences. The hierarchy motivates $k$-multinegative states, a generalization of binegative states and the associated $k$-multinegativity, which gives explicit upper bounds on both the asymptotic relative entropy and exact PPT entanglement cost. We construct states which are $k$-multinegative at arbitrarily large depths $k$ that separate consecutive levels of the previously introduced entanglement-cost hierarchy, disproving its conjectured finite collapse. These results provide state-dependent single-letter formulas while identifying a limitation of universal finite-level characterizations.

</details>


### [103] [Proof of a positive coherent-error threshold for topological quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20708)
*Shiro Tamiya,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了在相干Z旋转误差下，具有有界逻辑量子比特数的量子低密度奇偶校验码（含表面码等拓扑码）存在正的容错阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有阈值分析主要针对随机误差模型，而实际设备中的误差（如控制不完善导致的非期望Z旋转）可以表现为相干误差，无法被随机误差模型刻画。对表面码虽有数值研究表明相干误差下仍存在阈值行为，但缺乏严格的阈值存在性证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将傅里叶分析与抽象聚合物模型的集团展开相结合：傅里叶分析用于保留相干误差各振幅之间的干涉效应，集团展开用于处理聚合物模型，从而对最大似然泡利恢复的性能进行严格分析。

Result: 证明了对于具有有界逻辑量子比特数的量子低密度奇偶校验码（包括表面码及其他拓扑码），在相干Z旋转误差下存在正的阈值。具体地，当旋转角低于一个与码尺寸无关的常数时，最大似然泡利恢复使纠缠不忠实度随码距指数衰减，其前置因子与物理量子比特数成线性关系。

Conclusion: 该结果为相干误差下的量子纠错阈值存在性提供了严格证明，拓展了量子纠错的理论基础，并给出了超越随机误差情形的量子纠错的统计力学描述。

Abstract: Threshold analyses of quantum error-correcting codes are well established for stochastic error models, in which errors occur randomly with given probabilities. However, errors in actual devices can also be coherent, such as unwanted $Z$ rotations due to imperfect control, which are not captured by stochastic error models. For the surface code, numerical studies have indicated threshold behavior even under coherent errors, but a rigorous proof of threshold existence is lacking. Here we prove that a positive threshold for coherent $Z$-rotation errors exists for quantum low-density parity-check codes with a bounded number of logical qubits, including the surface code and other topological codes. Specifically, we show that the maximum-likelihood Pauli recovery suppresses the entanglement infidelity exponentially in the code distance up to a prefactor linear in the number of physical qubits whenever the rotation angles lie below a constant that is independent of the code size. The proof combines Fourier analysis to retain the interference among the amplitudes of coherent errors with the cluster expansion of abstract polymer models. Our results expand the theoretical foundation of quantum error correction and offer a statistical-mechanical description of quantum error correction beyond stochastic errors.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Simulation of Dissipative Non-Markovian Coupled Classical Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20721)
*Malte Schade,Sophia Simon,Nathan Wiebe,Scott Keating,Cyrill Bösch,Andreas Fichtner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模拟非马尔可夫耗散、时变材料特性的经典振子网络的量子算法，利用Prony级数近似记忆核、嵌入马尔可夫态空间并通过Hamiltonian模拟线性组合估计能量，查询复杂度为Õ(α_tot t/ε)；证明该能量估计任务最坏情况下为BQP完全，并给出局部耦合拓扑中指数加速不可能的Lieb-Robinson型界，从而在三维局部耦合阻尼振子系统中实现四次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将近期无阻尼谐振系统量子加速推广至粘声与粘弹介质，探索现实中含耗散振子网络及近似波动方程是否可实现实际量子加速，并厘清可加速的范围与经典/量子复杂度界限。

Method: 用Prony级数近似记忆核，将历史依赖动力学嵌入由非厄米算子支配的马尔可夫态空间；采用Hamiltonian模拟的线性组合（LCU）估计t时刻部分振子的瞬时动能与势能，误差为ε；并借助适用于微分方程的新型Lieb-Robinson类界分析局部耦合拓扑的可加速性。

Result: 算法以Õ(α_tot t/ε)次振子系统查询完成能量估计，α_tot为耗散强度、弹簧常数、逆质量与连接稀疏度的多项式；该能量估计任务最坏情况经典困难（对应判定问题BQP完全），强耗散下亦成立；时变材料属性在能量表示中表现为有效耗散或增长；证明局部耦合拓扑中指数量子加速不可行；三维局部耦合阻尼振子系统获得四次量子加速。

Conclusion: 将无阻尼谐振量子加速扩展至粘声/粘弹耗散振子网络，给出复杂度上界与BQP完全性下界，界定局部耦合拓扑中指数加速不可行，并为现实含耗散振子网络与近似波动方程提供四次量子加速的可能性。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for simulating classical oscillator networks characterized by non-Markovian dissipation and time-varying material properties, extending recent speedups for undamped harmonic systems to viscoacoustic and viscoelastic media. We embed the history-dependent dynamics into a Markovian state space governed by a non-Hermitian operator by approximating memory kernels through a Prony series. We then use linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation to estimate the instantaneous kinetic and potential energy for a subset of oscillators at time $t$ within error $ε$ using a number of queries to the oscillator system that scales as $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(α_{\rm tot} t/ε)$, where $α_{\rm tot}$ is polynomial in the strength of the dissipation, spring constants, inverse masses, and sparsity of the connections in the network. We further show that this energy estimation task is in the worst-case classically hard (i.e., a corresponding decision problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete), even in the presence of strong dissipation. For time-dependent materials, we show that changes in material properties appear as effective dissipation or growth in the energy representation. Additionally, we prove the infeasibility of exponential quantum advantages in locally coupled topologies through a novel form of Lieb-Robinson-like bounds that apply to differential equations. This allows our quantum algorithms to provide quartic speedups for locally coupled damped oscillator systems in three dimensions, raising the possibility of practical quantum speedups for realistically damped oscillator networks and approximated wave equations.

</details>


### [105] [$k$-fold unbiased measurements and maximal incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20728)
*Sébastien Designolle,Máté Farkas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将k-重无偏基概念推广到任意秩投影测量的k-重无偏测量(k-UMs)，建立了代数与谱k-UM理论，给出高阶秩一构造的不可能性结果，并利用Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数构造无穷多高阶秩三元组；同时给出基于测量不相容性的操作解释、精确的广义不相容鲁棒性及噪声阈值处的显式联合测量，并为sum-of-squares层级提供对称性约化的数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互相无偏基刻画了两个量子测量之间的完美互补性，但超越成对无偏性的高阶推广虽被提出，却几乎没有非平凡的高阶构造。作者希望把k-重无偏基从秩一基测量扩展到任意秩投影测量，以探索更高阶无偏结构是否存在更丰富的理论，并寻找其在测量不相容性中的操作意义。

Method: 定义k-重无偏测量(k-UMs)，发展代数k-UM与谱k-UM两个概念，并证明在秩一测量和三元测量(3-UMs)情形下二者一致；对高阶秩一构造及三结果3-UM建立强不可能性(no-go)结果；利用Hadamard矩阵和Clifford代数构造无穷多高阶秩三元组；在测量不相容性框架下给出精确操作解释；对任意结果数的3-UM精确求出广义不相容鲁棒性，并在相容阈值的噪声版本上构造显式联合测量；最后实现最近提出的sum-of-squares层级的对称性约化并进行数值分析。

Result: 证明代数与谱k-UM在秩一测量和3-UM情形下重合；得到高阶秩一构造与三结果3-UM的强no-go结果；借助Hadamard矩阵和Clifford代数获得无穷多高阶秩三元组；对任意结果数的3-UM精确确定广义不相容鲁棒性，并在相容阈值处构造噪声版本的显式联合测量；数值证据表明k-UM分析给出的噪声阈值可能刻画sum-of-squares层级的渐近行为，并以很高精度显示所构造的四结果3-UM属于最不相容的四结果测量三元组之列。

Conclusion: 将k-重无偏性从秩一基测量推广到任意秩投影测量可得到更丰富的理论；高阶无偏结构在秩一情形受到严格限制，但在高阶秩情形可通过Hadamard矩阵和Clifford代数大量构造；k-UMs与测量不相容性有紧密的操作联系，其噪声阈值可能反映sum-of-squares层级的渐近行为，所构造的四结果3-UM在数值上接近最不相容的四结果测量三元组。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases capture perfect complementarity between two quantum measurements. Extensions beyond pairwise unbiasedness have been proposed, but essentially no non-trivial higher-order constructions are known. We introduce $k$-fold unbiased measurements ($k$-UMs), extending the $k$-fold unbiased bases notion of [arXiv:1706.04446] from rank-one basis measurements to arbitrary-rank projective measurements, and show that this higher-rank setting supports a much richer theory. We develop the notions of algebraic and spectral $k$-UMs and prove that they coincide for rank-one measurements and triples of measurements (3-UMs). We establish strong no-go results for higher-order rank-one constructions and three-outcome 3-UMs, but obtain infinitely many higher-rank triples using Hadamard matrices and Clifford algebras. We then give these structures an exact operational interpretation in terms of measurement incompatibility. For 3-UMs with any number of outcomes, we determine their generalised incompatibility robustness exactly and construct an explicit joint measurement for their noisy versions at the compatibility threshold. Finally, we implement the symmetry reduction of the sum-of-squares hierarchy recently introduced in [New J. Phys. 28, 064509 (2026)] and give numerical evidence that the noise thresholds arising from the $k$-UM analysis may characterise the asymptotic behaviour of this hierarchy. In particular, with very high precision, we numerically show that our constructed four-outcome 3-UMs are among the most incompatible triples of four-outcome measurements.

</details>


### [106] [Blind Quantum Computation with a Small Quantum Server](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20729)
*Daniel Lovsted,Filipa C. R. Peres,Joshua Nevin,Selman Ipek,Anne Broadbent*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于泡利计算（PBC）的盲量子计算（BQC）协议，使服务器规模仅取决于非Clifford门数量，并给出基于纠缠的对偶协议以显著降低执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统盲量子计算中，服务器资源开销随计算规模增长，成为实际实现的瓶颈。作者希望降低服务器端资源需求，使低资源客户端能够安全外包量子计算。

Method: 利用泡利计算（PBC）框架设计BQC协议，使服务器规模仅依赖于非Clifford门计数；该协议继承PBC的容错性与量子比特虚拟化特性；并进一步构造基于纠缠的对偶协议，用于资源态计算。

Result: 所提出的协议实现了服务器资源规模与非Clifford门数量挂钩而非整体计算规模；证明即使不存在经典可模拟性，经典客户端BQC仍然可行；对偶协议显著降低了资源态计算的执行成本。

Conclusion: 该工作表明基于PBC的BQC可有效缓解服务器资源瓶颈，并拓展了经典客户端盲量子计算的可行性边界，同时提供了更高效的对偶实现方案。

Abstract: Blind quantum computation (BQC) allows low-resource clients to securely delegate computations to a quantum server, but server resource costs scale with the computation size, posing a bottleneck for implementations. By leveraging Pauli-based computation (PBC), we achieve BQC with a server whose size depends only on the non-Clifford gate count. Our protocol inherits fault tolerance and qubit virtualization from PBC and reveals that classical-client BQC is possible even in the absence of classical simulability. Finally, we present an entanglement-based dual protocol that performs a resource state computation with a dramatically reduced execution cost.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient Non-Uniform Quantum Hermite Transform through Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20739)
*Nitay Mayo,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文实现了前 N 个谐振子模式与 N 个加权位置空间采样之间的 Gauss-Hermite 正交变换，使用 O(N polylog(N,1/ε)) 个逻辑门与多对数级量子宽度，并在任意叠加态上给出包含辅助寄存器的算子误差界 ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算中，需要在谐振子模式系数表示与位置空间采样表示之间进行精确基变换，Gauss-Hermite 正交提供了这种精确变换，但如何以近线性门数和低量子宽度高效实现，并保证在任意叠加态上的误差可控，是待解决的问题。

Method: 利用 Gauss-Hermite 正交，将模式系数与 N 个加权位置空间采样建立精确基变换；构造中采用自适应窗口上的带符号平均，把均匀网格采样转换为加权 Hermite 根采样；通过控制窗口的不同宽度来管理放大的成本。

Result: 该变换可由 O(N polylog(N,1/ε)) 个逻辑门实现，量子宽度为多对数级；算子误差界 ε 在任意叠加态上成立，且涵盖所有辅助寄存器。

Conclusion: 通过自适应窗口带符号平均与可变宽度控制放大成本，作者得到了近线性的门复杂度与多对数量子宽度，为谐振子模式与位置采样间的高效精确变换提供了可行方案。

Abstract: On the span of the first $N$ oscillator modes, Gauss--Hermite quadrature gives an exact change of basis between mode coefficients and $N$ weighted position space samples. We implement this transform with $O(N\operatorname{polylog}(N,1/\varepsilon))$ logical gates and polylogarithmic quantum width. The operator-error bound $\varepsilon$ holds on arbitrary superpositions and includes all auxiliary registers. The construction uses signed averages on adaptive windows to convert uniform-grid samples into weighted Hermite-root samples. Their varying widths control the amplification cost, giving the near-linear bound.

</details>


### [108] [Mermin-Peres magic rectangles modulo odd primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20746)
*Josse van Dobben de Bruyn,Remy van Dobben de Bruyn,Peter Zeman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对每个整数 $d\ge2$ 构造了 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的线性方程组，它具有有限维算子解但无经典解，推广了 Mermin-Peres 魔方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mermin-Peres 魔方是 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ 上无经典解但有有限维算子解的线性系统的经典例子，而长期以来对奇 $d$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 如何构造类似例子未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对每个整数 $d\ge2$ 构造 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的线性系统。对奇素数 $p$，算子作用于两个 $p$ 维 qudit 上，通过向广义 Pauli 群添加对角多项式相位算子生成有限 $p$-群；经典不相容性由比较阿贝尔子群上赋值的初等线性论证得到。

Result: 对每个 $d\ge2$ 得到 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的线性系统，具有有限维算子解但无经典解；对奇素数 $p$，构造出作用于两个 $p$ 维 qudit 的算子解。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 Mermin-Peres 魔方式的量子-经典不相容现象推广到任意模数 $d\ge2$，特别是长期未解决的奇 $d$ 情形。

Abstract: The Mermin-Peres magic square provides a simple example of a system of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ which has no classical solutions but does have a finite-dimensional operator solution. For a long time, it was not known how to construct similar examples over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ with $d$ odd. In this paper, we construct, for every integer $d\ge2$, a linear system over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ that has a finite-dimensional operator solution but no classical solution. For an odd prime $p$, our operators act on two $p$-dimensional qudits and generate a finite $p$-group obtained by adjoining diagonal polynomial phase operators to the generalized Pauli group. Classical inconsistency follows from an elementary linearity argument comparing assignments on abelian subgroups.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Entropy Contraction and Factorization from Hypercontractivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20753)
*Li Gao,Lijun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明超压缩性蕴含熵收缩，无需细致平衡条件；对KMS对称本原量子马尔可夫半群给出修正Log-Sobolev常数与谱隙的定量关系，并应用于条件期望平均的超压缩性导出相对熵的近似张量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信道与量子马尔可夫半群的收敛性分析中，超压缩性与熵收缩（如修正Log-Sobolev不等式）之间的蕴含关系通常依赖细致平衡或L_p正则性等额外假设。作者希望去除这些限制，建立更一般的超压缩性到熵收缩的桥梁。

Method: 作者对单个量子信道直接证明超压缩性蕴含熵收缩，不假设细致平衡。针对关于忠实不变态σ的KMS对称本原量子马尔可夫半群，通过谱隙λ与‖σ^{-1}‖_∞建立修正Log-Sobolev常数α_1的下界，从而绕开L_p正则性假设。作为应用，分析两个条件期望的算术平均的超压缩性。

Result: 证明了超压缩性蕴含熵收缩；对KMS对称本原量子马尔可夫半群得到α_1 ≥ λ/(2+log‖σ^{-1}‖_∞)，其中λ为谱隙；去除了通过Log-Sobolev常数进行比较时对L_p正则性的假设；并证明两个条件期望的平均的超压缩性蕴含相对熵的近似张量化。

Conclusion: 该工作在不依赖细致平衡与L_p正则性的条件下，建立了超压缩性与熵收缩之间的定量联系，推广了量子Log-Sobolev理论，并为相对熵近似张量化提供了新的超压缩性判据。

Abstract: We prove that hypercontractivity implies entropy contraction for a single quantum channel, without a detailed balance condition. For primitive quantum Markov semigroups that are KMS-symmetric with respect to a faithful invariant state \(σ\), we obtain the modified Log-Sobolev bound $α_1\geq \fracλ{(2+\log\|σ^{-1}\|_\infty)}$ where $λ$ is the spectral gap. This removes the assumption of \(L_p\)-regularity for the comparison through the log-Sobolev constant. As an application, we show that the hypercontractivity of an average of two conditional expectations implies the approximate tensorization of relative entropy.

</details>


### [110] [Procrastinating einselection in non-Markovian quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20757)
*Michael J. Moody,Tara Kalsi,Agung Budiyono,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对有限维开放量子系统中的环境诱导选择（einselection），建立了可观测量的量子速度极限，并揭示了非马尔可夫性通过两种机制加速可观测量的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: einselection是开放与耗散量子动力学中的基础过程，描述了指针态的形成与经典性的涌现；在量子达尔文主义框架下，可通过观测量向指针值的运动来研究einselection。为此需要刻画可观测量的演化速度。

Method: 针对由可微时间局域开放动力学支配的有限维系统，推导了可观测量的量子速度极限。

Result: 识别出非马尔可夫性提升观测量速度的两种机制：一是由布居数保持或恢复所支持的直接耗散运动；二是通过恢复可用于相干运动的不对称性所产生的间接增强。

Conclusion: 对于永久einselection，累积的负性（negativity）会延迟指针基的出现。

Abstract: Among the processes governed by open-system and dissipative quantum dynamics, environment-induced superselection, or einselection, is particularly foundational, since it describes the formation of a preferred set of pointer states through environmental monitoring and ultimately the emergence of classicality. Especially in the context of Quantum Darwinism, einselection can be studied in terms of the motion of an observable towards its pointer value. To this end, we derive a quantum speed limit for observables for finite-dimensional systems governed by differentiable time-local open dynamics. We identify two mechanisms through which non-Markovianity can enhance observable speed: direct dissipative motion supported by population retention or revival, and indirect enhancement through restoration of the asymmetry available for coherent motion. For permanent einselection, this delays the emergence of the pointer basis due to accumulated negativity.

</details>


### [111] [Analytic leakage suppression with a single control field: fast two-qubit gates with tunable couplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20766)
*Lukas Heunisch,Michael J. Hartmann,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Magnus展开的简单解析脉冲整形技术，仅需单一基带控制通道即可有效抑制非绝热泄漏，在可调耦合器Transmon的快速iSWAP门中泄漏抑制达三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流解析脉冲整形方法（如DRAG和transitionless driving）通常需要两个正交控制通道，第二通道用于打破时间反演对称性，因此难以应用于仅有单一实值控制场的场景，例如超导电路中常见的基带磁通控制。作者希望解决这一限制。

Method: 通过Magnus展开推导出一种简单的解析脉冲整形技术，该技术仅依赖单一基带控制通道；并以可调耦合器实现的两个Transmon量子比特门为例，利用基带磁通脉冲进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 该修正显著降低了因耦合器调制引起的非绝热泄漏；在真实器件参数下，快速iSWAP门的泄漏被抑制最高达三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法具有通用性，不局限于抑制泄漏跃迁处的不需要的谱权重，可推广到多种量子计算平台，为单通道控制场景下的量子门优化提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Simple analytic pulse-shaping techniques are of great practical utility in quantum control, with prime examples being the DRAG method for suppressing leakage in superconducting microwave gates and the transitionless-driving approach to shortcuts-to-adiabaticity. Standard versions of these methods require two orthogonal control channels, with the second channel effectively breaking time-reversal symmetry. This appears to rule out their use in settings with only a single real-valued control field, such as the kind of baseband flux control that is common in many superconducting circuit architectures. We show here that a simple analytic pulse-shaping technique derived via a Magnus expansion is effective even with just a single baseband control channel. We demonstrate its efficacy by simulating a two-qubit gate between transmons realized with a tunable coupler and baseband flux pulses. Our corrections dramatically reduce non-adiabatic leakage caused by ramping the coupler: for realistic device parameters, leakage in a fast iSWAP gate is suppressed by up to three orders of magnitude. Our approach is general, goes beyond simply suppressing unwanted spectral weight at leakage transitions, and can be applied to a variety of platforms.

</details>


### [112] [All causally separable quantum processes are quantum circuits with classical control of causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20774)
*Julian Wechs,Alastair A. Abbott,Cyril Branciard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了多体因果可分性的充分条件同时也是必要条件，从而给出了完整刻画，并表明所有因果可分过程均可实现为经典控制因果序的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体情形下的因果（非）可分性此前已被提出和研究，但Wechs、Abbott和Branciard给出的因果可分性充分条件是否为必要条件尚属未解决的开放问题，本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 作者采用一种新颖的"相干隐形传态技术"（coherent teleportation technique）进行证明。

Result: 证明该充分条件同时也是必要条件，因而构成多体因果可分性的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 所有因果可分过程都可实现为"经典控制因果序的量子电路"，即操作间顺序由经典方式控制的广义量子电路。

Abstract: The concept of causal (non)separability describes whether the causal order between parties that perform local quantum operations is well-defined or indefinite. Causal (non)separability in the general multipartite setting was introduced and studied in [Oreshkov and Giarmatzi, New J. Phys. 18, 093020 (2016); Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)]. We resolve an open problem from these earlier works by showing -- using a novel "coherent teleportation technique" -- that a sufficient condition for causal separability identified in [Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)] is also necessary, and thus provides a complete characterisation of multipartite causal separability. A consequence of this result is that all causally separable processes admit a realisation as generalised quantum circuits in which the order between the operations is classically controlled, known as "quantum circuits with classical control of causal order".

</details>


### [113] [Asymptotically Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20780)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了量子比特上显式渐近好量子局部可测试码族：具有常速率、常相对距离、常权重局部测试器与常可靠性的显式量子LDPC CSS码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可测试码（qLTC）是量子PCP猜想与量子纠错的核心对象，但此前已知构造在参数上难以同时达到良好渐近性，尤其是基于量子比特（qubits）的显式构造稀缺。本文旨在弥补这一空白，给出参数全面最优的显式构造。

Method: 采用量子LDPC CSS码框架，通过显式代数/组合构造设计校验矩阵，使其满足：常速率编码、常相对距离、每个局部测试器作用在常数个量子比特上，且局部测试具有常数可靠性（constant soundness）。

Result: 得到显式量子LDPC CSS码族，其码率、相对距离、测试器权重与可靠性均为常数，从而在量子比特上实现渐近好且可局部测试的量子码。

Conclusion: 该工作首次（或显著推进）给出量子比特上参数全面的显式渐近好qLTC构造，为量子PCP猜想与容错量子计算提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes over qubits. More precseily, we construct explicit quantum LDPC CSS codes over qubits having constant rate, constant relative distance and constant weight local testers with constant soundness.

</details>


### [114] [Parallel quantum channel discrimination and numerical ranges in tensor product subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20781)
*Adam Bílek,Paulina Lewandowska,Ryszard Kukulski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行方案下的完美量子信道判别，提出结合二分法的SDP方法，以关于拷贝数线性的时间计算用于完美判别的量子态，给出了实现完美判别所需的最小信道拷贝数，并正面解决了Duan等人提出的Conjecture 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道判别是量子信息论的核心问题，其中"完美判别"（零错误判别）尤为重要，但如何高效构造判别所需量子态、以及最少需要多少个并行信道拷贝，此前尚缺乏系统解答，已有文献中的Conjecture 1也未获证明。

Method: 以数值域（numerical range）的概念与基本性质为工具：将判别问题转化为半定规划（SDP），并配合二分法搜索，从而在关于拷贝数线性的时间内计算出一个可实现完美判别的量子态；同时证明关键结论——矩阵子空间张量积的数值域最小角等于各子空间数值域最小角之和。

Result: 得到了计算完美判别量子态的高效SDP+二分算法（时间复杂度关于拷贝数线性），确定了实现完美判别所需的最小信道拷贝数，并据此正面证明了Duan、Guo、Li与Li提出的Conjecture 1，刻画了一类算子子空间实现完美判别所需的并行使用次数。

Conclusion: 数值域为并行完美量子信道判别提供了统一而有力的分析框架，其中数值域最小角在张量积下的可加性是核心工具；基于该框架的结果既给出了算法与最小拷贝数，也解决了此前的公开猜想。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination plays a crucial role in quantum information theory. Of particular interest is the case in which the channels can be discriminated perfectly. In this work, we focus on the perfect quantum channel discrimination task in a parallel scheme. We develop an SDP formulation combined with a bisection procedure to compute a quantum state for perfect discrimination in time linear in the number of copies. In addition, we obtain the minimal number of copies of quantum channels to achieve perfect discrimination. Thanks to that, we settle in the affirmative Conjecture 1 of Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016], which characterizes the number of parallel uses needed to discriminate perfectly a distinguished family of operator subspaces. All our results are obtained using the notion and basic properties of the numerical range. In particular, the key fact that we prove and use is that the minimal angle of the numerical range of a tensor product of matrix subspaces equals the sum of the minimal angles of the numerical ranges of the individual subspaces.

</details>


### [115] [Environment Alignment and Redundant Record Formation in Imperfect-CNOT Quantum Darwinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20823)
*Aleksander Lasek,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究不完美CNOT模型（随机高斯耦合、纯非相互作用环境量子比特）中，制备、相互作用角度与场无序如何共同影响量子达尔文主义的冗余记录形成，发现无场时单一对齐参数Λ决定条件态可区分性，并揭示场与不完美度对记录的非单调效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通过环境冗余记录解释客观信息，已有工作表明不完美记录可被放大、环境自演化可增强或抑制其形成，但制备、相互作用角度与场无序三者的联合作用尚不清楚，需系统研究其对可靠记录形成的影响机制。

Method: 采用带随机高斯耦合的不完美CNOT模型，环境为纯态、非相互作用量子比特。通过条件分支分离的几何分析、片段信息的精确表达式，以及最多24个环境量子比特的数值模拟，比较均匀局域场与等均方根强度高斯分布场的作用，并分析Holevo信息在Z基下的最优性。

Result: 无场时，单一对齐参数Λ决定制备与相互作用角度对条件态可区分性的依赖；增加相互作用不完美度或局域场强度既可提升也可抑制记录，呈非单调响应，可由条件分支分离的几何解释。纯初始环境下Z基对Holevo信息仍最优，故场辅助记录无需改变所记录的系统可观测量。无序在等均方根强度下拓宽了场的增益与损害效应。精确表达式与数值模拟刻画了高平均信息与跨片段、有限观测窗口内可靠记录之间的区别。

Conclusion: 研究将局域信息获取的几何与稳健记录所需的片段尺寸联系起来；分支可区分性分析仅依赖每个环境量子比特的条件演化，可推广至保持系统指针基的其他相互作用。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains objective information through redundant environmental records. Earlier work established that imperfect records can be amplified and that environment self-evolution can enhance or suppress their formation. We investigate how preparation, interaction angle, and field disorder combine in an imperfect-CNOT model with random Gaussian couplings and pure, noninteracting environment qubits. We find that, without fields, a single alignment parameter $Λ$ determines the preparation and interaction-angle dependence of conditional-state distinguishability. Increasing interaction imperfection or local field strength can improve or suppress recording. We explain this nonmonotonic response through the geometry of conditional branch separation. For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Universal Suppression of Dissipation across Conformal Interface in Open Quantum Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19292)
*Ruhanshi Barad,Tian Wu,Qicheng Tang,Xueda Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究含共形界面的局域耗散量子临界链的弛豫动力学，提出"模式分辨弛豫系数"以消除原有弛豫系数在边界条件依赖下的非普适性，并证明其在弱耗散区呈现普适的界面透射依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形界面是研究一维量子临界系统普适透射现象的重要平台；闭合系统中能量与信息透射可由普适量刻画，但共形界面在耗散动力学中的作用尚不清楚。近期基于Liouvillian gap定义的弛豫系数在部分边界条件下会因最小衰减率对应模式随界面透射改变而变得非普适，需要新的普适刻画方式。

Method: 引入模式分辨弛豫系数c_relax，通过连续追踪同一Liouvillian rapidity模式随界面透射的变化来定义；针对临界谐振子链与临界自由费米子链，结合解析与数值计算，在弱耗散区系统考察边界条件、耗散强度、局域耗散位置等微观细节的影响。

Result: 原有的Liouvillian gap弛豫系数在特定边界条件下会因主导衰减模式的切换而失去普适性；而c_relax在所考察的所有情形中均对界面透射呈现相同的普适依赖关系，不受边界条件、耗散强度与局域耗散位置等微观细节影响。对边界耗散情形，该普适行为在有限耗散强度下依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立了开放量子临界系统中跨共形界面的普适模式分辨弛豫刻画，为耗散动力学中的界面普适性提供了新的判据与框架。

Abstract: Conformal interfaces provide an important setting for studying universal transmission phenomena in one-dimensional quantum critical systems. While energy and information transmission across such interfaces are characterized by universal quantities in closed systems, the corresponding role of conformal interfaces in dissipative dynamics is less understood. In this work, we study relaxation in locally dissipative quantum critical chains with a conformal interface and show that a universal characterization emerges at the level of individual relaxation modes. We first show that the relaxation coefficient defined in the recent work [1] from the Liouvillian gap can become non-universal for certain boundary conditions because the mode determining the smallest decay rate can change as the interface transmission is varied. To resolve this ambiguity, we introduce a mode-resolved relaxation coefficient $c_{\rm relax}$ by continuously tracking the same Liouvillian rapidity mode as a function of the interface transmission. Using analytical and numerical calculations for a critical harmonic chain and a critical free-fermion chain, we find that, in the weak-dissipation regime, $c_{\rm relax}$ follows the same universal dependence on the interface transmission in all cases considered, independent of microscopic details such as the boundary conditions, dissipation strength, and location of the local dissipation. For boundary dissipation, this universal behavior persists even at finite dissipation strength. Our results establish a universal mode-resolved characterization of relaxation across conformal interfaces in open quantum critical systems.

</details>


### [117] [Collective Symmetry and Criticality from Single-Component Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19629)
*Chunhao Guo,Zhe Wang,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出用单一序参量场生成的Lee-Yang零点比值来提取集体对称性与临界信息，无需测量所有竞争序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物理中对称性对相分类和临界有效理论至关重要，但识别集体对称性困难：基于预设序参量的检验会遗漏隐藏分量。核心目标是从单一可及分量中反推对称性与临界信息。

Method: 利用单一有序场产生的Lee-Yang零点比值。未被测量的分量会塑造投影涨落分布，从而影响相对零点位置。结合解析推导与大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 在自发对称破缺相深处，各向同性序参量的零点比值解析地趋于由集体分量数目固定的无参数Bessel序列；在量子临界附近，涨落使该分布变形，比值偏离及其有限尺寸耦合依赖携带额外临界信息。在具有常规与扩大连续对称性的多体系统量子蒙特卡洛模拟中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法可在不测量每个竞争序参量的情况下，定量获取集体对称性与临界性。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a fundamental role in modern physics by guiding the classification of phases and the construction of effective theories of critical systems. Identifying collective symmetry remains challenging because tests based on preselected order parameters can overlook hidden components. A central goal is therefore to extract symmetry and critical information from a single accessible component. We pursue this goal using Lee--Yang zero ratios generated by a single ordering field. Unmeasured components shape the projected fluctuation distribution and hence the relative zero positions. Deep in a phase with spontaneous symmetry breaking, we show analytically that the zero ratios for an isotropic order parameter approach a parameter-free Bessel sequence fixed by the number of collective components. Near quantum criticality, fluctuations deform this distribution. The resulting ratio deviations and their finite-size coupling dependence carry additional critical information. We demonstrate these results with large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations of many-body systems with conventional and enlarged continuous symmetries. This approach provides quantitative access to collective symmetry and criticality without measuring every competing order.

</details>


### [118] [Encounter Propagator for Multiple Targets: A Dirichlet-to-Neumann Spectral Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19995)
*Denis S. Grebenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将遭遇（encounter）式表述推广到带多目标的受限扩散：分别追踪每个目标上累积的边界局部时，通过把Dirichlet-to-Neumann（DtN）算子分解为按目标限制的块，绕开DtN特征基不使局部时依赖对角化的困难，得到收敛的切换级数、等价的算子型更新方程，并对双目标给出显式谱表示与Bessel函数重求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目标遭遇式受限扩散已有成熟处理，但多目标情形下各目标边界局部时必须分别区分；此时目标投影算子一般与DtN算子不对易，导致DtN特征基无法对角化局部时依赖，直接推广受阻。作者旨在建立可解析、可数值近似的多目标框架，并用于理解目标间反复转移及扩散控制反应。

Method: 1）建立多目标遭遇式表述，分别定义各目标上的边界局部时；2）将DtN算子分解为目标限制块（target-restricted blocks），处理投影算子与DtN的非对易性；3）通过多维Laplace反演得到收敛的切换展开（switching expansion），各项对应目标间交替扩散转移，并导出等价的算子值更新方程（renewal equation）；4）双目标情形：对角化目标限制块，给出由两个DtN块谱与目标间耦合矩阵构成的显式谱表示；5）当耦合保持谱模时，将切换级数用修正Bessel函数精确重求和；6）对分离良好的目标利用非对角DtN块的平滑性作低秩有限维近似，并分析小且分离目标的二模约化。

Result: 得到多目标受限扩散的收敛切换级数及等价算子型更新方程；双目标情形获得显式谱表示；耦合保持谱模时切换级数可精确重求和为修正Bessel函数形式；一般情况下可用低秩有限维近似处理；在小且分离良好的目标二模约化中识别出目标间反复转移被逐步抑制的机制（regime）；并讨论其概率解释及对扩散控制反应的意义。

Conclusion: 该工作为多目标受限扩散提供了基于边界局部时的系统遭遇式框架，克服了DtN特征基无法对角化局部时依赖的障碍，给出收敛展开、精确重求和条件与可操作的有限维近似，揭示目标间转移被抑制的条件，并为扩散控制反应提供新的解析与概率工具。

Abstract: We develop an encounter-based formulation of restricted diffusion in a bounded domain with multiple targets, resolving separately the boundary local time accumulated on each target. Unlike the single-target problem, the target projection operators do not generally commute with the governing Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) operator, so that its eigenbasis does not diagonalize the local-time dependence. We overcome this difficulty by decomposing the DtN operator into target-restricted blocks. Multi-dimensional Laplace inversion then yields a convergent switching expansion, whose successive terms describe alternating diffusive transfers between the targets. We also derive an equivalent operator-valued renewal equation. For two targets, diagonalizing the target-restricted blocks provides an explicit spectral representation in terms of two DtN block spectra and an inter-target coupling matrix. When the coupling preserves spectral modes, the switching series can be resummed exactly in terms of modified Bessel functions. In general, as the off-diagonal DtN block is smoothing for separated targets, one can resort to low-rank finite-dimensional approximations. We examine an effective two-mode reduction for small, well-separated targets and identify a regime in which repeated inter-target transfers are progressively suppressed. Probabilistic interpretations and implications for diffusion-controlled reactions are discussed.

</details>


### [119] [Higher-Order Interactions in Complex Systems: Mechanisms, Behaviour, Representation and Reducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20142)
*Francisco J. Pérez-Reche*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该综述区分了高阶相互作用中的"机制、行为与表征"三个问题，形式化了可约性概念，并综述了检测高阶依赖与重构相互作用结构的方法，强调观测数据本身通常无法确定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶相互作用能在复杂系统中产生爆炸性同步、不连续相变和稳定性改变等显著集体行为，但"多体表述"并不等于"多体机制"，因为行为与机制都依赖于系统的表征方式。作者希望澄清机制、行为、表征之间的混淆，并为判断高阶模型能否被低阶模型替代提供严谨框架。

Method: 这是一篇理论综述。作者将问题拆分为三问（机制、行为、表征），区分了直接多体机制、非线性但成对却模拟多体的机制、经共享环境或消去变量产生的间接路径，以及作为观测伪影的时间聚合。随后将可约性形式化为"源模型与某类可容许低阶模型之间的比较"，相对于给定目标（微观规则、可观测量或定性特征）和容差。并综述信息论与推断方法，用于检测高阶依赖、检验低阶替代模型与重构相互作用结构。

Result: 作者论证同一行为可由不同机制产生，某一高阶机制并无普适的行为后果；因此不能用行为反推机制。信息论与推断方法可作为互补工具检测高阶依赖并检验低阶替代，但仅凭观测数据一般无法确定机制。

Conclusion: 论文提出以"机制—行为—表征"三分框架和可约性形式化来统一高阶相互作用的研究，并指出未来开放问题：机制与可测量signature的关联、粗粒化、可辨识性与因果性，以及将归约推广到平均场之外。

Abstract: Higher-order interactions couple three or more elements in ways that cannot be decomposed into pairwise contributions. In diverse complex systems they produce striking collective behaviours such as explosive synchronization, discontinuous transitions and altered stability. Neither such behaviour nor a description written in many-body terms establishes a many-body mechanism, since both depend on how the system is represented. This review distinguishes three questions: what mechanism governs the system, what behaviour does it produce, and how is either represented? Representation covers both which variables are included and how relations among them are encoded. We examine direct many-body mechanisms, nonlinear but pairwise mechanisms that mimic them, and indirect routes through shared environments or eliminated variables, together with temporal aggregation, an artefact of observation rather than a mechanism. The same behaviour can arise from different mechanisms, and a given higher-order mechanism has no universal behavioural consequence. We then formulate reducibility, the question of whether a higher-order model can be replaced by a lower-order one, as a comparison between a source model and an admissible class of lower-order models, relative to a target and a tolerance. The target may be a microscopic rule, an observable or a qualitative feature. Information-theoretic and inferential methods are reviewed as complementary tools for detecting higher-order dependence, testing lower-order alternatives and reconstructing interaction structure. However, observational data alone generally cannot establish the mechanism. We close by identifying open problems in linking mechanisms to measurable signatures, coarse-graining, identifiability and causality, and in extending reductions beyond mean field. (Abridged)

</details>


### [120] [Landau diamagnetism and the de Haas-van Alphen effect from a single geometric construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20146)
*Sung-Hoon Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零温下的初等构造方法，通过将自由电子气占据态沿磁场方向逐区间归入朗道能级，用"两全等三角形间态转移"的质心算术直接导出朗道抗磁磁化率，并在费米面极端截面临域自然地给出dHvA振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道抗磁性由巨正则势导出，虽能得到正确磁化率但缺乏物理直观；而"极端截面对应dHvA振荡"这一规则常被断言而非被展示。作者希望给出一个既初等又直观的构造，同时把朗道抗磁性与dHvA振荡统一在同一图像中。

Method: 在零温下对自由电子气作态计数构造：沿磁场方向按区间把占据态分组，使其"凝聚"到相应朗道能级上。在每个区间内，场致能量代价归结为两个全等三角形之间的态转移，用质心（重心）算术求和得到朗道磁化率。对极端截面临域，改用直接的零温态计数来定位剩余贡献。

Result: 该构造在绝大部分区间给出朗道抗磁磁化率；仅在费米面极端截面的狭窄邻域失效。在该邻域内贡献以dHvA周期振荡，每有一个朗道能级通过极端截面即产生一次振荡。直接态计数表明残余贡献集中在最靠近极端截面的少数区间，远处区间之和在峰值振荡幅度尺度上可忽略。

Conclusion: 朗道抗磁性与dHvA振荡可由同一零温几何构造统一理解：前者来自全等三角形态转移的质心算术，后者来自极端截面邻域内少数区间的残余态计数。该图像将"极端截面支配dHvA振荡"从断言变为显式展示。

Abstract: Landau diamagnetism is usually derived from the grand canonical potential, a calculation that yields the correct susceptibility but little physical insight, while the rule that extremal cross sections govern the de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) oscillations is commonly asserted rather than exhibited. We present an elementary zero-temperature construction in which the occupied states of a free-electron gas are grouped, interval by interval along the field direction, into the Landau levels onto which they condense. Within each interval the field-induced energy cost reduces to a transfer of states between two congruent triangles, and center-of-mass arithmetic yields the Landau susceptibility. The construction fails only in a narrow neighborhood of an extremal cross section of the Fermi surface; there the contribution oscillates with the dHvA period, one oscillation for each Landau level that passes through the extremal cross section. Direct zero-temperature state counting locates the oscillation: the residual is concentrated in the few intervals nearest the extremal cross section, the summed contribution of the distant ones being negligible on the scale of the peak oscillation amplitude.

</details>


### [121] [Universal 1/f Noise in the Power Spectra of Energy Time-series in Solvated DNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20391)
*Harsh Sahu,Deepika Sardana,Pramod Kumar,Ajay Kumar Chand,Sobhan Sen,Sanjay Puri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过MD模拟研究三种DNA构象（G-四链体、发夹、双链）的能量涨落，发现DNA自相互作用、DNA-水、DNA-离子能量时间序列均呈1/f噪声，衰减指数普适约为β≈0.90；DNA拓扑与结构刚性通过调控其与水和离子的动态耦合，塑造了噪声谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同DNA构象的能量涨落及其与周围水化层、可移动抗衡离子的动态耦合如何影响噪声行为，从而理解DNA拓扑结构与环境耦合在生物相关DNA构象随机动力学中的作用。

Method: 采用全面的分子动力学（MD）模拟，对G-四链体、发夹和双链三种DNA构象的DNA自相互作用、DNA-水、DNA-离子能量时间序列进行1/f噪声分析并提取衰减指数。

Result: 三种能量时间序列均呈1/f噪声，衰减指数普适，约为β≈0.90；柔性结构（G-四链体、发夹）的DNA自能量表现出明显的低频模式，而刚性双链中缺失；DNA拓扑与结构刚性调节其与水和抗衡离子的动态耦合。

Conclusion: DNA拓扑与环境耦合的相互作用决定了生物相关DNA构象中不同的随机行为，DNA的柔性和拓扑通过调制与溶剂及离子的动态耦合显著影响1/f噪声谱。

Abstract: We investigate energy fluctuations of solvated DNA in three conformations: G-quadruplex, Hairpin, and Duplex. Our comprehensive molecular dynamics (MD) simulations demonstrate that the time-series of interaction energies for DNA self, DNA-water and DNA-ion show 1/f noise with a universal decay exponent $β\simeq 0.90$. We show that DNA topology and structural rigidity modulate its dynamic coupling with the surrounding hydration shell and mobile counter-ions. This coupling critically shapes the 1/f noise profile: DNA self energies in flexible structures (G-quadruplex, Hairpin) display pronounced low-frequency modes absent in the rigid Duplex. These findings reveal how the interplay between DNA topology and environmental coupling governs different stochastic behavior in biologically relevant forms of DNA.

</details>


### [122] [Khinchin's ergodicity and typicality in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20433)
*Dario Lucente,Marco Baldovin,Giacomo Gradenigo,Angelo Vulpiani*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Khinchin的观点，认为统计力学成立的关键仅在于系统自由度数目大和选择广延观测量，而无需动力学混沌，并通过对谐振链和Toda模型的研究加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs系综方法在平衡态统计力学中极为成功，但其成功原因仍有争议。通常推导统计力学主要结论需假设动力学具有遍历性，然而经验观察显示即使这些假设不成立，理论预测仍有效，因此需要重新审视统计力学有效性的真正前提。

Method: 作者采纳Khinchin的观点，即统计力学有效性只依赖于系统自由度数量大以及选取广延观测量，与微观动力学细节无关，也不要求存在动力学混沌。为此，他们对两个经典可积系统——谐振链和Toda模型——进行了解析与数值研究。

Result: 研究发现，即使缺乏混沌，系统仍表现出许多平衡态统计力学所预测的重要特征，例如Maxwell-Boltzmann分布。

Conclusion: 统计力学的有效性并不依赖于动力学混沌或遍历性假设，自由度大与广延观测量的选择才是关键，这支持了Khinchin的观点。

Abstract: The formulation of statistical mechanics in terms of ensembles, originally proposed by Gibbs, has proved to be extremely effective in describing the equilibrium state of many-particle systems, in the most diverse contexts (critical phenomena, quantum mechanics, biophysics, just to mention a few). The reasons of this success are still debated, and the connection between dynamics and probability in large systems remains somehow elusive. Indeed, in order to derive the main results of equilibrium statistical mechanics, strong assumptions on the ergodicity of the dynamics are usually required. Nonetheless, empirical observations seem to suggest that the predictions of the theory hold true even when such assumptions are not verified. In this paper we reconsider the point of view put forward by Khinchin, stating that the only relevant ingredients for the validity of statistical mechanics are the large number of degrees of freedom in the system and the choice of extensive observables, irrespectively of the details of the microscopic dynamics. In particular, the presence of dynamical chaos is not required. To this aim, we discuss some analytical and numerical results on a couple of classical integrable systems, the harmonic chain and the Toda model, showing that many important features predicted by equilibrium statistical mechanics, as for instance Maxwell-Boltzmann distribution, are found even in absence of chaos.

</details>


### [123] [Formal Fluctuation-Response Relations for Non-Stationary Systems: The Dynamic Conjugate Variable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20525)
*Igor M. Sokolov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简洁方法推导非平稳动力学中的涨落-响应关系（FRR），其核心是引入一个与扰动共轭的两时间变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涨落-响应关系连接系统对外部扰动的线性响应与未扰动自发涨落性质，但已有结果多限于平稳情形，需给出非平稳动力学下统一且简明的推导。

Method: 基于标准思路，引入与扰动共轭的变量（动态共轭变量）来推导FRR；仅依赖底层动力学的马尔可夫性，并额外假设过程转移概率密度对恒定、时间无关扰动的强度可微。该共轭变量为两时间形式，在平稳情形下退化为通常的单时间共轭。

Result: 论证该共轭变量结构与两时间特征，并说明若干已知结果可作为该方法推论而自然得到。

Conclusion: 该推导框架简洁且假设较弱，能为非平稳涨落-响应关系提供统一解释，并涵盖已有结果。

Abstract: Fluctuation-response relations (FRRs) connect the linear response of a system to an external perturbation with properties of spontaneous fluctuations in the unperturbed system. We provide a simple derivation of FRRs for non-stationary dynamics following an almost standard way based on introducing a variable conjugated to perturbation (a dynamic conjugate). The derivation relies solely on the Markovianity of the underlying dynamics, with the only additional assumption that the transition PDF of the process is differentiable with respect to the strength of a constant, time- independent perturbation. The structure of this conjugate variable is however unusual: it is a two-time one, and reduces to a usual single-time conjugate in stationary situations. We show how several known results follow from this approach.

</details>


### [124] [Martingale theory for heat and phase-space contraction in heterogeneous diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20681)
*Jing Qin,Nariya Uchida,Édgar Roldán*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究多维非平衡过阻尼Langevin动力学中热耗散的鞅性质，给出瞬态鞅/下鞅/上鞅的定量判据，揭示停时热统计与相空间收缩泛函之间的联系，并以受重力、双电层力及壁面流体动力学相互作用影响的球形微粒为实例，经数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机能量学多关注热耗散的平均值与涨落定理，而对热耗散过程本身随时间演化的统计结构（如是否为鞅）缺乏系统刻画；同时输运系数可显含时间/空间依赖、漂移具空间非线性的一般情形下，热涨落统计与非平衡非线性特征之间的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 针对输运系数显式依赖时间与空间、漂移含空间非线性项的一般多维过阻尼Langevin动力学，推导热耗散的鞅性质（鞅、下鞅、上鞅）的定量判据；建立停时（如首达时）热统计与刻画相空间收缩的泛函之间的定量联系；并将理论应用于受重力、双电层力并与刚性壁面存在流体动力学相互作用的球形微观粒子，通过数值模拟检验。

Result: 得到判定热耗散呈现瞬态鞅（时间守恒）、下鞅（时间递增）、上鞅（时间递减）行为的定量条件；发现停时热统计量与相空间收缩泛函之间的紧密联系；对球形微粒模型的理论预测与数值模拟结果一致。

Conclusion: 研究拓展了随机能量学的适用范围，揭示了热涨落统计性质与非平衡过程非线性特征之间的非平凡关系。

Abstract: We investigate martingale properties of heat dissipated by generic non-equilibrium overdamped Langevin dynamics with multiple dimensions and transport coefficients that may depend explicitly on time and/or space. We find quantitative criteria that establish that heat dissipation may exhibit transient martingale (time-conserved), submartingale (time increasing), and supermartingale (time decreasing) properties in Langevin dynamics with drift coefficients with spatial non-linear dependencies. Furthermore, we reveal a tight link between the heat statistics at stopping (e.g. first-passage) times with that of a functional quantifying the phase-space contraction in the system dynamics. The theoretical results are used to predict the statistical properties of the heat dissipated by a spherical microscopic particle subject to gravity and double-layer forces and hydrodynamically interacting with a rigid wall, whose validity is confirmed by numerical simulations. These results extend the scope of stochastic energetics by revealing a non-trivial relation between heat fluctuations' statistical properties and the non-linear features of non-equilibrium processes.

</details>


### [125] [Global Minima of the Thomson Problem in a Disk: A Molecular Dynamics Approach with Fixed Border Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20777)
*Georgiy K. Lavrov,Eduard G. Nikonov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文结合淬火分子动力学与固定边界启发式方法，报告了圆盘约束下经典Thomson问题N=60、61、92、99的改进全局最小构型，能量均优于已知最优值，且结果高度可重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Thomson问题（圆盘内库仑排斥电荷的最小能量排布）在N=60、61、92、99等尺寸下已有报道的构型可能并非真正全局最小。作者旨在获得更低能量、更可靠的全局最小候选构型，并厘清其对称性与缺陷Voronoi结构。

Method: 将淬火分子动力学（QMD）方法与合作者Amore与Zarate提出的固定边界启发式（fixed-border heuristic）相结合，对N=60、61、92、99的圆盘约束Thomson问题系统搜索全局最小构型，并分析其Voronoi图、对称性及多次独立运行的可重复性。

Result: 获得能量E_QMD(60)=2159.3584240930、E_QMD(61)=2237.19264190、E_QMD(92)=5358.35353314、E_QMD(99)=6254.83029083，均优于此前最优值。N=60构型的Voronoi图与早前报道不同；N=61的C2对称性与N=99的D1对称性得到确认，与已知对称模式一致；N=92此前Voronoi图对称性较低，而本文解呈现清晰的D1（轴向）缺陷对称。

Conclusion: 所获构型在多次独立运行中高度可重复，是稳健的全局最小候选解；该方法能有效改进Thomson问题的已知最优能量并修正对称性认知。

Abstract: We report improved global-minimum configurations for the classical Thomson problem of $N=60$, $61$, $92$, and $99$ repulsive Coulomb charges confined to a disk. By combining the quenched molecular dynamics (QMD) method with the fixed-border heuristic introduced by Amore and Zarate, we systematically obtain configurations with energies $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(60)=2159.3584240930$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(61)=2237.19264190$, $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(92)=5358.35353314$, and $E_{\mathrm{QMD}}(99)=6254.83029083$, which improve upon the previously best-known values. For $N=60$, the Voronoi diagram of our configuration differs from the one reported earlier. The symmetries for $N=61$ $(C_{2})$ and $N=99$ $(D_{1})$ are confirmed and are consistent with the known symmetry patterns for these configurations. For $N=92$, whereas the previously reported Voronoi diagram has lower symmetry, our solution exhibits a clear $D_{1}$ (axial) symmetry of defects. The results are highly reproducible across multiple independent runs, providing strong evidence that these configurations are robust global-minimum candidates.

</details>
